# Saiyan Legacy — Version Status

**Current playable build:** 0.2.0 — "Goku Showcase", frozen copy in `builds/v0.2/`
**In development:** 0.3.0 — "Saiyan Saga" (main folder; in-game footer says *Version 0.3 (in development)*)
**Previous versions:** 0.1.0 frozen in `builds/v0.1/`
**Last updated:** 2026-10-19

**Documents:** `MASTER_SPEC.md` = what the game is supposed to become · `HANDOFF.md` = where development is and what happens next (start there in a new session) · `STATUS.md` (this file) = detailed factual status and testing record · `README.md` = how to run and use it. If they disagree, check the source code and tests, then fix the wrong document.

---

## Fused Zamasu, episodes 64–67: "Vegito" in the episodes' order (the played assault and the Saiyan Assault, the Spirit Sword exchange, the Half-Corrupted change, the Final Kamehameha charged and released); Trunks' block in "The Sword of Hope"; the Saiyan Assault in Free Battle — RESEARCHED (episode guides) + OWNER RECOLLECTION (labelled adaptation) · AUTOMATED-VERIFIED (focused) · BROWSER-STEPPED BY CLAUDE · NOT HUMAN PLAYTESTED (2026-10-19)

"Vegito" now follows episodes 64–66: the merge; a played assault (judgment, a lockable Lightning Bird, then the Saiyan Assault team rush on a cue — alternating blows, two slowed, caught and thrown: the blows and slow motion an adaptation, the outcome the episode's); the turn (Rage Trunks and the Galick Gun, Vegeta's shield, the Kamehameha struggle, the swelling, Kaioken and the halo, the Light, the Potara, "Alright!" unchanged, Blue, the fingers); the Spirit Sword as a timing exchange (prompt in the lunge's last frames; misses retry; auto after three); FIGHT 2 opening on his tears and the Half-Corrupted change, growing as he weakens; the Final Kamehameha charged (mash) and released (Ultimate), the split, Zamasu still standing. Replaces the earlier "Saiyans' charge" placement (it sat after the halo beat). "The Sword of Hope" opens on Trunks blocking the god's blade. Free Battle: Saiyan Assault (Blue Goku with Blue Vegeta; after "Vegito"). Shared fixes: rush hits can slow time (`slow`); `K.judgment` counts only its caster's telegraphs (a stall on skip). Research reference: `documentation/FUSED_ZAMASU_EPISODES_64_67.md`. Open: `ft_scythe` plays the Mafuba before the scythe (episode 64 has the reverse).
Evidence: rewritten Vegito test (103 assertions, watched and skipped, checkpoints), new Spirit Sword exchange (12) and Saiyan Assault (14) tests, Sword of Hope with the block; the judgment fix mutation-checked; moveset 26/26, playtest3 15/15, sim filtered 73/73, `validateAll` 0; bot probes won with 0 errors; browser frames of every new beat. The full suite not run. Details: `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` 9.30.

## Roadmap Three closed out: the owner's decision list, the full-suite command, focused validation (2026-10-18) — NOTHING NEW BUILT · ONE NEW TEST + TARGETED TESTS + BOT PLAYTHROUGHS (CLAUDE) · NO BROWSER CHECK THIS STEP · FULL SUITE NOT RUN (NOT AUTHORISED) · NOT HUMAN TESTED

Every open item of the research PDF was checked again against its text and the source: none can be built without the owner's choice, so no beat was added. The seven decisions (Kaioken ×3 or ×4; day or night for the Galick clash; who Rosé skewers first; the scythe and the clones; the Mafuba; the Sword of Hope's cut; Beerus' Hakai), with the file, what the game does now and whether it is safe without the answer (it is, for all seven), plus the other brief-vs-PDF differences: `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` → **9.16**.

**Changed:** `tests/moveset-tests.js` only — a new test of S-END-04's split renderer (two and three panels tile the canvas at five sizes and four pixel ratios, the size changing between frames; the seams black; the canvas state given back; `Battle.render` draws the split only while it is set; disposing mid-split gives back the frame, all three auras and Goku's teal; mutation-checked: it fails without the one-pixel overdraw, reproducing the defect 9.15 fixed, and without the scissor reset); the S-END-04 test also checks Jiren's aura now. HANDOFF.md's *How To Test* said the full suite takes "about two minutes": corrected (the last runs took 32 and 44 minutes).

**Checks run (source manifest `47dfd27d…ca71`, 393 files; command in 9.16):** `moveset-tests` filtered 18/18 (652 assertions); `cell-games-tests` all 22 (449); `sim-tests` filtered to the skip and hand-off tests 12/12 (263); `playtest4` all 38 (369); `frieza-fair` all 12 (58); `validateAll` 0; bots watched and skipped of ten missions (20 runs, all won, 0 engine errors). **Full-suite command:** `node tests/run-tests.js` (`--fast` skips every suite this work touched). **Not run:** the full suite (not authorised). **Not checked:** the browser in this step (`renderer.js` unchanged since the previous entry's browser checks), full speed, a human playtest; Vegito's Potara chime is still headless only.

## The research PDF re-audited beat by beat with its images; eleven more beats built (2026-10-18) — IMPLEMENTED · TARGETED TESTS + BOT PLAYTHROUGHS + FRAME-STEPPED BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN (NOT AUTHORISED) · NOT HUMAN TESTED

The whole PDF was re-read, its eight embedded images included (extracted locally), and all fifteen blocks were compared beat by beat with the code. The per-block table (verified / not taken with the reason / blocked) and every build: `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` → **9.15**. Every build is a research draft, not checked against the footage.

**Delivered (in the document's order):**
- *The Emperor's True Power* (S-FRZ-03): in the first Kaioken rush the cross snaps Frieza's head aside and the kick lands, each on a cut close beside him at a Dutch angle, a white flash frame and a hard jolt.
- Cell Games (GC-S03, GC-S04): the regrowth seen low from the foot of the vapour, the lens tilting up it as the green light climbs (non-graphic); "I give up" on a slow push-in.
- Gohan's SSJ2 (GS-S03, B-TR-04): the column from low on the stone, pulling hard back to 28 m; his eyes held low, then snapped up onto Cell (the shot also fixed: it ended inside his hair).
- The goodbye (SAC-S01, SAC-S02): Cell's laughing face on a slow push-in with blue crackle over the swelling; the soft smile at the two fingers.
- *Ultra Instinct* (MUI-S01): the eye close-up pushes in; the eyes shut, then open on a high chime.
- *The Final Battle*: Frieza's gold **fails** at the barrier (the owner's "gold failing", the PDF, all six reference images) — his white final form from there on (confirmed by the owner, 2026-10-18); the promise exchange is a split frame (CS-END-02); S-END-04's split now has the reference image's third panel, Jiren straining across the bottom.
- *Vegito*: a high chime as the Potara earrings go on (headless only).

**Renderer (shared):** the split's last pixel column was left undrawn at a fractional pixel ratio (700 px at 1.25) — fixed; resize, disposal, the no-push run and the skip all end with the whole frame (checked).

**Tried and not kept, with the reason (9.15):** Block 1 S05 straight down (he is lost under the dust); GS-S05's streak (the owner's executions arrive by vanish); SAC-S02's profile (the hand or the arm hides the face).

**Checks run (final source):** `moveset-tests` filtered 19/19 (274 assertions; 4 new tests, 1 extended); `sim-tests` filtered 15/15; `playtest4` filtered 8/8; `cell-games-tests` filtered 8/8; `frieza-fair` 1/1; `validateAll` 0; bots watched and skipped of nine missions (18 runs, all won, 0 errors); browser frames of every new shot and the renderer cases. **Not run:** `node tests/run-tests.js`. **Not checked:** full speed, a human playtest.

## Block 11 of the research: S-END-04's split frame (2026-10-18) — IMPLEMENTED · TARGETED TESTS + BOT PLAYTHROUGH + FRAME-STEPPED BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN (NOT AUTHORISED) · NOT HUMAN TESTED

In *The Final Battle*'s final push, when Frieza shouts "Go... OVER!", the screen splits down the middle for 1.3 s: Frieza's red eyes on the left, Goku's teal eyes on the right, both faces turned to a black seam, both shaking hard; Goku's pupils drop to black twice for a few frames while his hair stays gold. Then the full frame comes back and the push goes on as before (a fast push now holds at the edge until the split has played). New engine support: `SL.Render.renderSplit` (`src/render/renderer.js`) and a `battle.split` branch in `Battle.render()` (`src/game/battle.js`, CRLF kept); the shot itself is in `src/data/missions/top_finale.js`. Details and checks: `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` → **9.14**. A research draft, not checked against the footage.

**Checks run (final source):** new `moveset-tests` *S-END-04* test (23 assertions, watched, skipped and with nobody pushing; two parts mutation-checked); *CS-END-03* and *Vegito Blue* with it, 3/3; bot watched and skipped (won, 0 errors); `validateAll` 0; browser frames of the split, a flicker frame and the full frame after it; no console errors. **Not run:** `node tests/run-tests.js`. **Not checked:** full speed, a human playtest, window shapes other than 1024 × 768.

## Block 4 of the research completed: Krillin, the Super Saiyan awakening and the first exchange, CS-S01 to CS-S07 (2026-10-18) — IMPLEMENTED · TARGETED TESTS + BOT PLAYTHROUGH + FRAME-STEPPED BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN (NOT AUTHORISED) · NOT HUMAN TESTED

*The Legendary Super Saiyan* (`src/data/scenes/namek_super_saiyan.js`) now follows the research's shot table where it agrees with the owner's nine shots. Krillin is held in a purple-pink sphere, seen from low behind Goku's silhouette as the lens pushes in (CS-S01). The fists are shot in profile, the lens tilting up to his bowed head (CS-S03). At the burst a column of white-gold light erupts from beneath him, seen low from his side as the lens pulls hard back (CS-S04). Then a cut to his face: the eyes shut, then snap open on the teal (CS-S05). Frieza's first rush is panned side-on (CS-S07). CS-S02 stays off (the owner's non-graphic choice). Details and checks: `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` → **9.12**. A research draft, not checked against the footage.

**Root cause fixed (the near-white burst):** the scene started the transformation without `noShots`, so the engine's own face close-up cut in 14 frames after the burst, 1.5–2 m from him inside the flash (95–99 % white pixels). The scene now owns the camera through the change; the change beat keeps the engine's front orbit, as before. After the fix: 33 % white pixels at the first burst frame, about 13 % after. The explosion sphere (6 m, it reached the lens) was replaced by the pillar.

**Checks run (final source):** `playtest4` filtered 6/6 (86 assertions; the Block 4 test has 14, two added this session and each mutation-checked); `sim-tests` filtered 5/5 (61); `frieza-fair-tests` *Namek phases* 1/1; `moveset-tests` *brush-off* 1/1; bot watched and skipped (won, 0 errors); `validateAll` 0; browser frames of every shot, and a skip during the change (control handed back, no story shot left). **Not run:** `node tests/run-tests.js`. **Not checked:** full speed, a human playtest.

## Block 2 of the research completed: the Galick clash's own camera, S-CLASH-03 / S-CLASH-04 (2026-10-18) — IMPLEMENTED · TARGETED TESTS + OUTCOME PROBE + BOT PLAYTHROUGH + FRAME-STEPPED BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN (NOT AUTHORISED) · NOT HUMAN TESTED

*The Saiyan Prince*'s beam struggle now has its own camera, in place of the engine's clash shot (this mission only; `src/data/missions/saiyan_vegeta.js` P.galick). As the two waves go out, an extreme wide shot is taken side-on to the line between the fighters (S-CLASH-03). It holds into the first moment of the collision. ## Roster rush audit; the rush camera and the catch fixed for every rush (2026-10-18) — IMPLEMENTED · TARGETED TESTS + HEADLESS ROSTER PROBE + FRAME-STEPPED BROWSER CHECK (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN (NOT AUTHORISED) · NOT HUMAN TESTED

Every rush technique (136 across 83 characters; 132 measured headless, 4 skipped: the two team rushes and the two counter-only Perfect Counters) was played and measured for length, staging, blows landed, one-frame jumps, camera gaps, under-ground frames, the end state and the recovery. The table, the verdicts and the open proposals are in `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` → **9.8**.

**Delivered (shared, `src/game/fighter.js`):** (1) in a rush, every cinematic shot except the finish's is held until the next shot replaces it, and a held shot ends with the rush or when it is cut off — before, the hit-stop stretched rushes past their shots, and 21 of the 30 cinematic rushes fell back to the gameplay camera mid-rush for 6–34 frames; after: 0. (2) A caught fighter is drawn into the blow position over five frames, not yanked in one frame (worst 1.41 m, Hirudegarn; after 0.45 m). Across all 132 rushes nothing else changed: blows, length and end state are the same run for run. No missing blows, stuck holds, under-ground frames or broken recoveries were found anywhere. **Test:** `tests/moveset-tests.js` new test *the roster rush audit*.

**Inspected, not changed:** a CPU Vegito Blue's long rushes on the player (no cinematic or impact frame, by design; with the gameplay camera both fighters are on screen 94–100 % of frames, like the existing rushes). The 20 one- and two-blow rushes are short by identity and are not to be lengthened automatically. An escape from long rushes is a proposal only (three options; recommended: a burst out at the vanish steps, reusing the existing burst escape) and needs the owner's decision.

**Checks run:** new test 1/1; `moveset-tests.js` alone 73/73 (fixed engine, before the new test was added); filtered tests that play rushes in other files: `playtest3` 1/1, `sim-tests` 2/2, `cell-games` 1/1; the roster probe before and after; browser: Super Dragon Twin Fist's held shot mid-rush, its finish, and the camera's return. **Not run:** `node tests/run-tests.js`. **Not checked:** full speed, a human playtest.

The owner handed over *Fight researches saiyan legacy from gemini.pdf* (92 pages; Roadmap Three's step 1) with the master execution directive. The reading record, the beat-by-beat mapping onto the existing missions, the rule used to decide what to build, and the open source questions: `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` → **9.4**. The research is a Gemini **draft** (it did not watch the footage; wrong episode numbers, invented monologues, some contradictions), so everything built from it is marked in the code as "a research draft, not checked against the footage". Where it conflicts with the owner's own briefs (Namek, the Cell Games, the ToP, *Broly*), the briefs win.

**Recovered first:** a previous session had started on this PDF after the last handoff and stopped at its usage limit without recording anything. Its work, checked and kept: `saiyan_vegeta` B04 (the Kaioken assault through the spires) and B-CLASH-05 (the beam carrying Vegeta into the sky), `namek_finale` CS-P3-04 (the silent look down), `ft_black` BLK-S01 (the tilt-up reveal) and BLK-S02 (`gb_handStudy`), a sim-test assertion for the two Chapter 3 beats. Its own results then: Chapter 3 sim 3/3, `whatif` 17/17, `playtest3` 15/15, `frieza-fair` 12/12, `playtest4` 34/34, bot playthroughs won.

**Built this session (each a research draft):**
- *The Saiyan Prince* (`saiyan_vegeta.js`): **B01** the first exchange staged before the duel is handed over — Goku closes in a straight line, a right straight; Vegeta leans out of it (new pose `v_leanBack`, `vegeta.js`) and hooks the ribs; Goku spins into a low sweep and Vegeta hops off it. **B03** the first Kaioken in the duel draws Vegeta's shocked line.
- *Rosé* (`ft_rose.js`): **BT-BLK-03** the Rosé change shot the research's way through the shared transform's `changeShots` — close on his face with the eyes shut, violet rings rising off his feet, a fast orbit as the hair goes pink, white rings over his head, then the eyes open on a smile and he names the colour. **BT-BLK-04** a first cut before the thrust: gone, behind Goku, the blade comes down, Goku turns into it with his guard up, and the cut fells a building across the street.
- *Vegito* (`ft_vegito.js`): the Spirit Sword **lifts** Zamasu on the blade (`vg_swordUp`); after the Final Kamehameha a short **false victory** (the shoulders rolled, a line) before the fusion gives out.
- *The Sword of Hope* (`ft_hope.js`): Zamasu throws a dark sphere at Trunks and the Sword of Hope comes down through it before the leap. The cut's direction is unchanged (still down; an open question).

**Bugs found and fixed:**
- **Held shots never moved (camera).** A mission shot's `t` is elapsed / duration; on a held shot (999 s) it stays ~0, so every held shot written as if `t` were seconds never moved. Found on the previous session's Black tilt-up (stuck on his boots) and Kaioken-assault pull-back. Fixed there, and then everywhere: **56 held shots in 23 missions** converted to elapsed seconds. Most were written in seconds (`t / 0.9`, `t * rate`); the seven written for a 0..1 `t` (`U.lerp(a, b, t)` — `namek_end` ×3, `namek_kaioken20`, `saiyan_raditz`, `saiyan_training`, `whatif_tail`) now run over the hold they sit in (1.4–4 s). Those camera moves never ran before, so they are new on screen (the ToP, Fusion Reborn, the Broly / Buu fusion dances, Hit, Namek's end, Universe 6). Browser-checked: `top_jiren`'s tilt up the pillar of light.
- **A story transformation's directed cut was overridden (`fighter.js`).** The engine's non-sequence "full" transform path played its own orbit shot even when the story owned the camera (`sd.noShots`); the sequence path already checked it. One guard, the same as the sequence path's. It only changes a story transformation that passes `changeShots` for a form without a `sequence`.

**Checks (this session):**
- Bot playthroughs, watched and skipped, all won: `saiyan_vegeta`, `ft_black`, `ft_rose`, `ft_vegito`, `ft_hope`; after the shot conversion `bb_fusion`, `buu_fusion`, `buu_kibito`, `fr_gogeta`, `hit_contract`, `namek_end`, `namek_kaioken20`, `rof_finale`, `saiyan_kingkai`, `top_finale`, `top_kefla`, `top_mui`, `top_saiyans`, `top_vegeta`, `u6_cabba`, `u6_goku_hit`, `whatif_tail`. **Bot losses, all pre-existing** (identical outcome, to the second, with the converted shots reverted in a scratch copy of the repository — a camera function cannot change the outcome): `fr_veku` (watched and skipped; its survive phase; no test covers it), `whatif_gogeta` (watched and skipped), `whatif_raditz` (watched), `top_jiren` (skipped). These are the generic bot at seed 3 (`play.js`), not the suites' own runs.
- Narrow suites (before the shot conversion): sim Chapter 3 3/3, `playtest3` 15/15, `whatif` 17/17, `wardrobe` 26/26, `moveset` 68 passed / 1 failed — the one failure was the expected building count on *Rosé* (the first cut fells a second building). The test now counts both and still requires the throw's one (it passes on the final source — re-run in the continuation session, Future Trunks Saga 5/5); new assertions for B01, the first cut, the Rosé change, the lift, the false victory and the sphere.

- *The Sword of Hope* (`ft_hope.js`): halfway through the gathering a cut from behind Goku and Vegeta frames both watchers and Trunks with the light rising into his sword. The red lasers were swallowed by the stage fog (linear 60–380 m — their midpoints were ~250 m away) and mostly hidden behind the central tower: now within ~150 m, either side of the tower, thicker with a pale core. The tilt-up camera sat inside Trunks' head (his hair filled a corner): moved beside him.
- **Shared fix (`hud.js`):** a form change made by a story scene (input locked) no longer wakes the HUD or shows the form banner over the scene (the erasure's Super Saiyan put "SUPER SAIYAN" and the bars over the sky shot).

**Roadmap One — the gaps the audit found, built:**
- **Final Flash** (`vegeta.js`): charge now decides strength — ready at 70 frames exactly as before (5000, 1.9 m), held to 108 the fully charged tier (6000, 2.4 m) with its own presentation (square on him at full, side-on with a short slow-motion at the release). Engine (`fighter.js`): cine event `full` on a beam's last tier; step filters `minTier` / `maxTier`.
- **Super Kamehameha** (Adult Gohan): a `cine` (three-quarter, the hands, side-on at the release and again for the push) and the second push (engine: `beamFire` reads `sp.push { at, radius, damage }`, cine event `push`): a head still travelling at frame 8 widens 1.25× and hits 1.1× harder.
- **Adult / Ultimate Gohan:** Perfect Guard counters for both (base: catch-and-elbow, rising knee; Ultimate: behind-him palm, gut punch); Ultimate Gohan's own heavies (double hammer, lifting palm). Like Vegeta's counters, they apply in the story too.
- **Death Ball** (Final Form Frieza) and **Solar Kamehameha** (Perfect Cell): `cine`s.
- **Perfect Counter** (Perfect Cell, game-original, labelled): an adaptation rush started only from a Perfect Guard (Heavy) — engine: a form's `moveSet.counter[kind].special` starts that extra; the extra's `counterOnly` keeps it off a cold press (the wheel shows "Perfect Guard, then Heavy"). Free Battle after `cell_fatherson`; Super Perfect Cell inherits it; the story's Cell fights keep their old heavy.
- **Stardust Breaker** (Fusion Reborn Gogeta, Super Saiyan): new technique `stardustBreaker` (`techniques.js`) on the existing prism orb and glitter (`SL.Dimension`): palm up, the orb grows over it, the fist crushes it, the dust is thrown into him (homing), glitter over him; a small violet burst rather than a white sphere. Free Battle after `fr_gogeta`; the film's staged one untouched.
- **Powerscaling** (Super Vegito vs SSJ4 Gogeta, by evidence): before — Super Vegito won 40/40 CPU duels (`tools/balance_duel.js`, seeds 1 and 7, new tweak `superVegito`), ~3× the damage he took; the roster audit showed him the strongest non-Blue form (×3.6 damage, ×2.4 defense) and SSJ4 Gogeta tuned like a base fighter. Changed: Super Vegito's form multipliers (damage 1.9 → 1.55, defense 1.6 → 1.3, speed 1.35 → 1.28), SSJ4 Gogeta's stats (power 2.2 → 3.0, defense 1.5 → 2.0, speed 1.42 → 1.66). After — 20/40 each, damage within 10 %. `buu_supervegito` and `buu_inside` still won by the bot, watched and skipped. Found, not changed: other single-form "already transformed" fighters (Blue Gogeta, SSJ4 Goku) sit far below the form-switchers of their tier — the owner's call for a roster-wide pass.
- **Roster audit:** all 131 playable fighters have Special 1 / 2 / Ultimate, a light string and heavies, no duplicate names; `validateAll` 0 (one problem found on the way and fixed: Super Perfect Cell inherited the new extra with Perfect Cell's form id).
- **Cell Saga cinematics (kept item):** *Goodbye Goku* (2026-10-01) and *Father-Son* (2026-10-14) read side by side — the farewell theme returns in Gohan's despair, both open on the smoke over the same spot; no change without the owner's notes.

**Test-suite debts (the first full run in many sessions found them; each traced to its cause first):**
- `tests/run-tests.js`' fast loader missed 15 newer character files — the long-standing `char_cooler` failure: added in `index.html`'s order. `--fast` **51/0** (was 50/1).
- Tests left pinned to designs the owner has since replaced — updated to the newer design, each stricter or as strict, none loosened: the lab scenario *Super Saiyan plays the full transformation event* (`tools/lab.js`; since the ToP refinement, 2026-10-13, a player's Super Saiyan is the practiced change — now asserts the clench, the burst, the reveal and *no* darkened sky or rage pose); `sim-tests` *the Super Saiyan scene…* (the 2026-10-14 nine-shot rebuild circles him once, at the change — now asserts that, the whisper before it and the close on his eyes after it); `playtest4`'s two (the same rebuild moved the send-away after the change and rewrote the quiet realization as the whisper); `rof-finale`'s order (Chapter 18 opens on Botamo since 2026-10-11 — predicted in STATUS then); `sim-tests` *the Majin form is a story decision* (Vegeta's form list gained Blue Evolution — now also asserts it is locked on a fresh save).
- `playtest2` *every attacking technique… lands* failed on Android 17's Tournament Barrier, a defence since the ToP refinement (the test's own comment said so): listed with the other defences, and a **new test** checks what the Barrier does — a blast bursts on the shell, the one who swings into it is pushed off and hurt, the shell is gone when it drops.
- **Left failing, pre-existing, owner's call:** `playtest2` *Saibamen: a pack…* — "shook it off as the player": traced — after the bot switches into the latched Piccolo, the latch ends ~20 frames later through the Saibaman's own state exit (an ally's hit knocks it off), so the player never mashes free; whether allies may knock a latched Saibaman off is a design decision (the owner's playtest note behind the test was "too easy to switch").

Everything else, including `sim-tests` (with `moveset-tests` now wired in), every mission's checkpoint sweep and the five Chapter 19 missions, passed.

## *Vegito* and *The Sword of Hope* refined to the owner's fight breakdown (2026-10-17) — IMPLEMENTED · HEADLESS PLAYTHROUGHS (BOT) + BROWSER GLANCE (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN TESTED

The owner's brief *Vegito vs Fused Zamasu: implementation + safe handoff* (a shot-by-shot breakdown of Vegito Blue's entrance, the fight, the Sword of Hope and Zeno's erasure). Short session by design (the owner's usage was at 79 %): one focused pass, then this handoff.

**Verified first (source read, not assumed):** all five Chapter 19 missions exist and are wired (`story.js` `super_future`, `index.html`, the fast loader, the bot brains); `ft_vegito` fuses through `SL.Fusion` both ways; God Slicer is opened only by `ft_vegito` (`openExtras`); the blade mesh, the sky apparition (raised, enlarged) and the double-sided erasure sphere in `ft_hope` are as the last entry says.

**Changed in `src/data/missions/ft_vegito.js`:**
- **The entrance:** Vegito is now born in his **base** form (`SL.Fusion.fuse` without a form), takes the confident stance (`vg_yosha`, "Alright!"), says his line, then his **Blue is staged** (`blueReveal`: low in the street looking up as the aura climbs white to cobalt, divine sparks, a small blue burst and a soft flash so the reveal stays readable, the smirk, "Now — Vegito Blue."). Vegito Blue has no transformation sequence of its own and Free Battle is untouched (the staging lives in the mission).
- **The fight in two played stretches with a staged beat between:** FIGHT (played, **in the sky**, `space: 'air'`, ends at 65 % of Zamasu's bar) → WRATH (staged: Zamasu raises **Holy Wrath**, throws it down, Vegito **catches it and drives it back** into him; Zamasu comes down onto the **Spirit Sword** — Zamasu's chest is kept on a point along the actual blade so it reads as through him, no wound shown — and a short taunt, paraphrased) → FIGHT 2 (played, ground and sky, to 35 %) → FINAL (unchanged: crossed arms, the Final Kamehameha, the fusion running out, Half-Corrupted Zamasu). Phases `fusion, fight, wrath, fight2, final`; two scored stretches.

**Changed in `src/data/missions/ft_hope.js`:** Goku and Vegeta (base, spent) now stand behind Trunks watching the energy gather; red light rains from Zamasu's sky onto the city during the apparition (adapted).

**Source fidelity (honest):** the episodes were **not** checked this session (no source access was used, to save the owner's budget). From memory and **unverified**: Vegito appears and then goes Blue (now matches); the Spirit Sword wounding Fused Zamasu; the fusion ending early around the Final Kamehameha (the mission keeps that order). **From the supplied breakdown, unverified, kept as adapted:** Holy Wrath caught and pushed back; "Almighty Vegito Blue" (not used — a paraphrase instead); the red lasers. **Discrepancy:** the breakdown has Trunks thrust into the stomach and cut *upward*; the original roadmap PDF says the giant sword comes *down* in a vertical arc. The mission keeps the downward cut — to be checked against the episode. **Not represented:** Mai, the surviving humans and children (no such cast in the project), the "30-foot" Spirit Sword (the prop is ~2 m; the slash streak suggests more).

**Checks (exact, on the final source):** bot playthrough of `ft_vegito` watched (77 s) and skipped (18 s) — won, phases in order, flags `vegitoBase, vegito, caught, wrathBack, impaled, fight, fight2, finalKame, defused, corrupted`, no beam left, camera released, no engine errors (run before the last Spirit Sword placement edit; the moveset test below ran after it). `node <scratch one-suite runner> tests/moveset-tests.js "Future Trunks Saga"` — **5/5** (before the Spirit Sword placement edit), then `… 'mission 4 "Vegito"'` — **1/1** after it; `universe6-tests` **8/8** after it. The moveset test now also asserts the base→Blue order, the Holy Wrath beat, the five phases, two scored stretches, the watchers and the lasers. Browser (port 5175, `startMission('ft_vegito', { startPhase: 2 })`): the Spirit Sword beat — first framing showed the blade pointing up beside him (found, fixed), then the blade through his torso holding him up; no new console errors (one logged crash came from Claude's own wrong `startTraining` call — a form id where the setup screen passes an index — not from the game). **Not run:** the full suite; the rest of `moveset-tests` after these edits; `playtest3/4`, `rof-finale` (untouched by this pass). **Not browser-checked:** the Blue reveal, the Holy Wrath catch, the watchers, the lasers.

## Free Battle / Training partner (2v2) and the first team rush, Unlikely Alliance (2026-10-17) — IMPLEMENTED · NARROW TESTS + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN TESTED

The owner: built-in cooldowns stay off; continue with the blocked prerequisites, starting with the two-attacker rush and a tag / 2v2 mode (roadmap #43).

### Done

1. **Partner and Second Opponent** (setup screen `screens_select.js`, launch `main.js`): new rows **Partner** (None or any unlocked fighter; the arrows or the portrait grid), **Partner Form** (the same unlock rule as Starting Form) and, in Free Battle, **Second Opponent** (None or a fighter, base form). A partner is a CPU ally on your side at the battle's CPU difficulty and joins you in a **party** (the existing hero switching: the party key — Z / D-pad right — hands you control of the partner and back; when your fighter goes down you take over the partner). The battle already ended only when a whole side was down, so 1 v 2, 2 v 1 and 2 v 2 work as they are. In Training the partner cannot die; the HUD's training panel now reads the dummy from the other side (it took the first other fighter, which could be the partner); Training's reset puts a partner back beside you. "None" in both keeps the old one-on-one.
2. **Engine — the team rush** (`fighter.js`): a rush spec `partner: { ids, forms, name, range, pose }` needs that ally on your side, close and free (`Fighter.teamPartner`); without one the wheel says "Needs … at your side" (`extraLock`). At contact the partner is pulled in — an afterimage where he was, then on the **far side** of the held one (a `scripted` state held by the rush, positioned every frame); steps with `by: 'partner'` are the partner's blows (from his pose library; the damage is his), `h.selfPose` what the leader does meanwhile, and `h.partnerKi: { pose, color, damage }` the partner's shot fired with the leader's last blow (his lands first). The partner goes back to fighting on his own when the combo ends, the rush state is left for any reason, or he is hit out of it (which also ends the rush); a partner taken out before contact means no rush (the held one is let go). Schema: partner steps are checked against the partner's poses; `by` and `partner.ids` validated.
3. **Unlikely Alliance** (the PDF's Tournament of Power team rush, Goku and Golden Frieza against Dyspo — a game rush): on **Goku** (Super Saiyan Blue, with Golden Frieza as partner) and, led the other way, on **Frieza** (Golden, with Blue Goku); earned by clearing *The Final Battle* (`top_finale`); Free Battle / Training only; 60 ki. Frieza's cage of light (ki lines all round him), Goku's low kick, Frieza's tail, two finger lasers, Goku kicks him back into them, then both at once — the Kamehameha (a point-blank beam) and the Death Beam (Frieza leading: the Death Beam pierces, Goku's Kamehameha alongside). Camera: track, wide on the three, impact.
4. **Found and fixed:** a pose's `hands` array is **[left, right]** (`model.setHands(l, r)`); eleven poses written this session had the open / pointing hand on the wrong side (Final Impact's raised hand showed a fist, Godly Execution's palms, Dirty Fireworks' fingers, the God Shot, the silver hook, the brush-off, Ultimate Gohan's palm, …) — all fixed; *Prince of Saiyans*' ring scene now uses Final Impact's raised-hand pose. Older poses that look written the same way (`v_palmExecute`, `v_offerHand`) were not touched.

### Checks

`moveset-tests` 55/55 (+4: 2 v 2 — four fighters, a two-member party, the partner a CPU ally, switching to him, one opponent down and the fight goes on, both down and your side wins; Unlikely Alliance led by Goku and by Frieza — usable with the partner beside you, "at your side" when he is 80 m away, who strikes each blow `goku frieza goku frieza frieza frieza goku frieza goku` / `frieza frieza goku frieza frieza frieza goku goku frieza`, the partner on the far side through the rush, let go after, the three shots, camera back; the partner hit out mid-rush ends it cleanly; a partner knocked down before contact means no rush). Roster-wide schema 0 problems. Narrow suites: in the run recorded below. Browser (port 5175, `SL.Dev.unlockAll` in the page): the Free Battle setup screen shows Partner / Partner Form / Second Opponent and fits; `startFreeBattle` with Blue Goku + Golden Frieza against Vegeta + Nappa → four fighters, party Goku / Frieza, Unlikely Alliance on the wheel; frame-stepped: Goku sweeping on one side, Golden Frieza on the far side, Vegeta between; at the end Frieza free, camera back, Vegeta down and the fight going on against Nappa; no console errors.

### Gaps / for the owner

- Not human-tested. The setup screen's right side still shows two cards (you and the opponent), not the partner's or the second opponent's.
- The partner and second opponent are CPU at the battle's difficulty; only the partner can be switched to. Story missions are unchanged (their own parties).

## Shared tasks from the roadmap: the bright flash on heavy hits, the audio voice cap (2026-10-17) — IMPLEMENTED · NARROW TESTS + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN TESTED

Two of the four shared engineering tasks the roadmap kept separate from the moves (plan 8.13 / 8.17), each with its cause investigated first.

- **Bright flash on close heavy hits.** Cause: `battle.js` `hitEffects` whitened the whole screen with `ScreenFX.flash(0.12 × impact)` on **every** hit of impact 4–5 anywhere in the battle (0.48–0.6), and `vfx.impact` lit the ground with a point light of intensity 2 + level reaching 12 + 6·level m (6–7 over 36–42 m) under every impact of level 3+. Now: the screen flash only for a hit the player is in, at 0.05 × impact (0.2–0.25); the impact light 1.2 + 0.5·level reaching 8 + 3·level m, 0.16 s. The accessibility setting *flash reduction* still cuts the screen flash to a quarter. Browser (Final Impact's blast, the frame that was white): both fighters and the field readable, `ScreenFX.flashValue` 0.04 two frames after, the impact lights 1.7 and 2.2. Not touched: the explosions' own flash sprites (`vfx.explosion`, opt-in `opts.flash` since slice 6).
- **Audio voice cap.** Causes found in `audio.js` `play`: the voice counter went up **before** the sound function ran and never came down if it threw (each throw kept a voice for good — enough of them and every one-shot was dropped for the rest of the session); the cap was first come, first served (a burst of punches could swallow the finisher); a context the browser suspended mid-game was never woken. Now: the counter goes up only after the sound is made; everyday fight sounds (`MINOR_SFX`: swing, punches, block, dodge, dash, jump, small ki blasts and explosions, menu moves) give way at 40, everything else (finishers, beams, big explosions, voices of moves) has 12 more voices of reserve; a suspended / interrupted context is resumed (that one sound is lost). Browser (the real AudioContext): 60 punches at once → 40 voices, a big explosion still plays (41), back to 0 after 2.5 s.
- **Checks:** `moveset-tests` 50/50 (+1 audio test in an isolated context: 100 failing sounds → 0 voices; at the cap a punch gives way, a finisher plays; the reserve ends; a suspended context resumed). Narrow suites after the flash change: `feel` 18/18, `immersion` 15/15, `frieza-fair` 12/12 (+ the rest in the session's last run). Not human-tested: whether the heavy hits now feel too soft is the owner's call (the two numbers are in one line each).
- **The CPU uses wheel rushes** (`controllers.js`): a new `wheelRush` choice in the generic AI — a wheel extra of type `rush` the CPU fighter could use itself right now (its form, its unlocks, the ultimate rule, ki, cooldown) from inside what the rush's dash covers, weighted like a Special and more when the target is open; it obeys the existing fairness rules (halved before the reaction time against a charging target, cut against a committed ultimate, cut while resting). **Outside the story only** (story fights keep their tuned kits). Headless: a CPU Majin Vegeta with the moves earned used Final Impact 3× in 50 s (seed 3) and Atomic Rush 3× (seed 11), none on seed 7 — it is a weighted choice — and none in story mode. Other wheel types (beams, `beamSurf`, God Bind) stay player-only unless a hand-written brain uses them.
- **Cooldowns of the built-in types (beam / rush / projectile): not changed — the owner's call.** Turning them on is one line in `_trySpecial`, but **82 existing specs across the roster carry a `cooldown` that has never applied** (Frieza's Death Beam 150 frames, Dabura's, Frost's, the Androids', Cooler's…, story bosses included): enabling it is a balance change for all of them, not a move task.
- **Files:** `src/game/battle.js`, `src/render/vfx.js`, `src/core/audio.js`, `src/game/controllers.js`, `tests/moveset-tests.js`.
- **Session's last run (final source, after every change of the day, each suite alone):** `moveset` 51/51, `feel` 18/18, `immersion` 15/15, `frieza-fair` 12/12, `cell-games` 22/22, `rof-goku` 8/8, `buu-c` 19/19, `buu-b` 16/16, `fusion-reborn` 4/4, `super-broly` 12/12, `trunks-arrival` 13/13, `buu-ultimate` 7/7, `universe6` 8/8, `whatif` 17/17, `character-pass` 10/10, `wardrobe` 26/26, `buu-chamber` 5/5; `rof-finale` 4/5 — the same pre-existing campaign-order assertion (`u6_botamo`). `graphify update .` run. Full suite (`node tests/run-tests.js`) **not** run.

## Movesets and ultimates, slices 6–8: Majin Vegeta's Atomic Rush and Final Explosion, Vegeta's era rushes, Goku's form rushes (2026-10-17) — IMPLEMENTED · NARROW TESTS + FRAME-STEPPED BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN TESTED

The owner's *Execute the entire 23-page moveset roadmap* brief (no more approval questions; keep going slice by slice). The PDF was read in full this time (scratch text extraction; plan section 8 intro and 8.16). Every move below is a Free Battle / Training wheel extra (`freeOnly`): its own id, form gate, mission gate (`freeUnlock`), ki cost, damage per blow, hit reactions, `cine`, interruption handling. Names: the PDF's or the games' where it gives one, otherwise labelled game-original in the data.

### Engine (shared, opt-in per move)

- **Rush steps** (`fighter.js` rush state): `to: 'behind' | 'above' | 'below' | 'front'` — he vanishes (afterimage, `vanish`) and strikes from that side of the held one, who then stays where he is (the attacker keeps that side, faces him, never under the ground); `lift: m` — the step carries both up that far (a juggle; the held one is no longer treated as grounded); `move: true` — a step with no blow (only its pose and `to` / `lift`); `bind: { color }` — no blow: ki rings pulse on his wrists and ankles for the step (cine event `bind`); `slashes: n` — strike-lines across him at the blow; a `ki` blow's `beam: { length, radius, frames, core }` — a point-blank beam drawn from the hand along the shot for its frames, disposed at its end or when the rush state is left; `sp.detonate: { at, damage, radius, color, pose }` — the ki left in him bursts `at` frames after the last blow, wherever he is (fingers-up pose 10 frames after), and not if the rush is cut off first. Schema rules for each (`to` values, a beam only on the last ki blow, a no-blow step never last, a detonation shorter than the recovery).
- **Charged burst** (`techniques.js` `bomberWave`, opt-in look fields): `grow` (the aura and shaking swell through the charge — through `sd.aura`, which the old boss version's one-off `aura.target` never reached), `bolts` (lightning round him), `blastFlash` (scales the burst's flash sprites; `vfx.explosion` takes `opts.flash`), `sp.selfCost` (share of his maximum health it costs, never below 1 HP); the burst fires the cine event `release`; the state is a cine state now.
- **Camera** (`battle.js`): `novaShot` (cine `nova`: far off to one side and above, outside a burst of the move's reach, drawing back as it swells).

### Moves

| Move | Who / forms | Earned by (Free Battle) | Ki | Blows (reactions) | Camera |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Atomic Rush** (PDF heading; blows adapted from Majin Vegeta vs Goku) | Vegeta, Majin | *Prince of Saiyans* `buu_pride` | 45 | grab, roundhouse 700 (`hitSideL`), **rings pin him** 34 frames (no blow), jab 300, hook 320, gut 380, headbutt 1200 (heavy) | track, wide, over his shoulder at Vegeta (`reverse`), impact |

### Checks

`moveset-tests` 37/37 on the final slice-8 source (+12 since slice 5: Atomic Rush / Final Explosion gates and the ultimate rule; Atomic Rush on Goku and Kid Gohan — pinned ≥ 30 frames with no blow, ≥ 24 rings, its six reactions, five poses in order, sent away, its four shots, camera back; Atomic Rush cut off while he is pinned — the rings stop, nobody held, camera back; Final Explosion at 8 m (caught once) and 60 m (untouched) — all ki, half his health, aura swelling, ≥ 20 lightning bolts, its four shots, free after, camera back; Final Explosion cut off in the charge — no burst, no health spent, aura and camera back at once; Vegeta's five era rushes: gates (unearned / other form / story) and each landed — its blows, poses in order, shots, carried up ≥ 2–3 m where it says so, the last blow from above / behind where it says so, one beam drawn and disposed; Dirty Fireworks cut off before the burst → no burst; a beam cut off mid-shot is disposed; schema for `to`, beam and detonate; Goku's seven form rushes: gates and landed the same way, incl. strike-lines and the beam from below; Angry Kamehameha lands with its two shots). Narrow suites after the slice-6/7 engine: `feel` 18/18, `immersion` 15/15, `frieza-fair` 12/12, `cell-games` 22/22 (bomber users), `rof-goku` 8/8, `buu-c` 19/19, `fusion-reborn` 4/4 — run while the slice-8 engine fields (`move`, `slashes`, the grounded fix for anchored lifts) were being added; re-run on the final source is in the next slice's checks. Browser (port 5175, Training, `SL.Dev.unlockAll` in the page, paused and hand-stepped): Atomic Rush — the rings on Goku's wrists and ankles from over his shoulder; Final Explosion — the crouched gather in the orbit shot, then (after a fix, below) the burst as a dome of ki with debris and Goku thrown out of it, end state clean (free, camera back, mood cleared, half health, aura back to idle); Final God Strike — Vegeta above firing the beam down onto Goku; Meteor Combination — the Kamehameha from beneath into Vegeta. No console errors.

**Found and fixed while checking:** Final Explosion's first release shot (`pull`) started inside the 11 m fireball — a white frame; replaced by `nova` from outside the reach and the burst's flash sprites scaled down (`blastFlash`). Dirty Fireworks' burst hit had no reaction pose (`undefined`) — now `hitHeavy`. Lifting a held target that stays in place left him flagged grounded, so he never rose — fixed.

### Gaps / for the owner

- **Not human-tested; full speed not seen by Claude** (the pane runs the loop slowly; all looks are hand-stepped). Damage and ki are first guesses.
- **The white frames on heavy finishers are the shared bright-flash issue, cause now pinned:** `battle.js` `hitEffects` calls `SL.ScreenFX.flash(0.12 × impact)` on every hit of impact 4 or more, on top of `vfx.impact`'s point light (level ≥ 3). The finishers above keep impact 4–5 like the earlier rushes; the shared task decides the fix (not patched per move).
- Instinct Surf does not surf a live enemy beam (needs the beam's state); its dodging is the form's existing evasion. Final Explosion's charge is armoured (escape it, do not trade with it); the CPU does not use any of these extras (player-only, as before).
- Rushes that end high in the air leave the attacker floating there (as every rush in the engine; fliers).

## Free Battle / Training Starting Form follows the form unlocks (2026-10-17) — IMPLEMENTED · NARROW TESTS + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN TESTED

Found during slice 5 (below) and done at the owner's request ("Do this task here"). The setup screen's **Starting Form** (and the opponent's / dummy's form) listed every form that was not `storyOnly`, earned or not — a new save could start as Mastered Ultra Instinct Goku or Blue Evolution Vegeta, although the in-battle transform rule (`formFreeUnlocked`) said such forms exist outside the story only once their mission is cleared.

### Done

1. **One rule, shared** (`fighter.js`): `SL.formOpenFree(form)` — outside the story a form is open when it is not `storyOnly`, not `storyLocked` unless it also names a `freeUnlock`, and earned (`formFreeUnlocked`). The Fighter constructor now uses it for its outside-the-story locks (the same rule as before, one copy instead of three lines); `SL.formStartFree(form)` = open, or a story-locked form with **`freeStart: '<mission>'`** once that mission is cleared — a starting form only, never on the transform button.
2. **The setup screen** (`screens_select.js` `formOptions` / `startForm`): only `formStartFree` forms are listed; a remembered starting form that is no longer listed falls back to Base (as story-only forms already did). Both rows (yours and the opponent's / dummy's) follow it; `SL.Dev.unlockAll` still opens everything earned-gated.
3. **The three story-locked forms that have no `freeUnlock`** (owner's decision, 2026-10-17): Vegeta's **Majin** is a starting form once *The Mark* is cleared (`freeStart: 'buu_majin'`; keeps Final Impact reachable) and stays off the transform button; Vegeta's **Super Vegeta** and Piccolo's **Fused** (Nail) are **story-only** — not starting forms at all, even after their missions (Claude had first given them `freeStart` too; the owner chose story-only and the field was removed). The other 28 unlockable forms already name a `freeUnlock` and now simply wait for it in the row too.

### Checks

`moveset-tests` 25/25 (+1: a new save's row has Base / Kaioken / Super Saiyan for Goku and none of MUI, Blue, Blue Evolution, Majin, Super Vegeta, Fused or Gogeta's story-only Blue; MUI after `top_mui`; Majin and SSJ2 after *The Mark* with Majin still not a transformation; Super Vegeta and Fused still absent after `cell_perfect` and `namek_nail`; story-only never; every `freeUnlock` / `freeStart` names an existing mission) — re-run 25/25 after the owner's story-only change. Each alone, final source: `buu-chamber` 5/5, `buu-ultimate` 7/7, `universe6` 8/8, `super-broly` 12/12, `whatif` 17/17, `fusion-reborn` 4/4, `buu-c` 19/19; `rof-finale` 4/5 — the failure is the campaign-order assertion ("rof_goku,rof_finale,u6_botamo" !== "…u6_cabba"), story order data untouched here and predicted in this file on 2026-10-11 (*Universe 6 rounds* → "expect existing order assertions … rof-finale-tests.js to need the new neighbours"). Browser (port 5175, the real Training setup screen): with every mission read as unplayed (an in-page stub of `SL.Save.missionResult`, nothing written) Goku cycles Base / Kaioken / Super Saiyan, Vegeta Base / Super Saiyan, Piccolo Base; on that origin's test save (those missions cleared) Goku all eleven, Vegeta eight incl. Majin and Super Vegeta, Piccolo Base / Fused; Vegeta → Starting Form: Majin → START TRAINING: Majin Vegeta, wheel Galick Gun · Big Bang Attack · Final Flash · Final Impact · Power Down, Majin still locked for the transform button; no console errors. After the story-only change, on the same cleared save: Vegeta cycles Base / SSJ / SSJ2 / God / Blue / Majin / Blue Evolution (no Super Vegeta), Piccolo Base only; no console errors. (The setup screen was left on Goku / Base on the 5175 origin's test save; the owner's 5173 save was not opened.)

### Gaps / for the owner

- **A visible change for existing players:** forms not yet earned no longer appear as starting forms. If the owner wants Free Battle to stay "everything open", say so — it is the two filters in `screens_select.js`.
- Super Vegeta and Fused Piccolo were selectable starting forms before this change (unearned); they are now story-only by the owner's choice.
- Not human-tested. Full suite not run (the constructor change touches every fighter's form locks; the narrow suites above cover the story's and Free Battle's form gates).

**Files:** `src/game/fighter.js`, `src/ui/screens_select.js`, `src/data/characters/vegeta.js`, `src/data/characters/piccolo.js` (comment only in the end), `tests/moveset-tests.js`; STATUS, HANDOFF, README, `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md`.


## Movesets and ultimates, slice 5: the master roadmap recorded, Majin Vegeta's Final Impact, a skill-wheel pointing fix (2026-10-17) — IMPLEMENTED · NARROW TESTS + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN TESTED

The owner's brief *Master cinematic moveset, rush attack and story moment roadmap* (pasted text; the attached *saiyan legacy most saiyan ultimates.pdf* could not be rendered on this machine — no PDF renderer — so the pasted text was the source). One bounded slice: record the whole roadmap, verify the current state, build the next step (brief Phase B: Majin Vegeta).

### Done

1. **Roadmap recorded:** `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` section 8 — every idea of the brief (48 numbered items, the story moments, the arena table, the reliability, wheel and unlock rules, the A–G order), each with what the code has today, checked against the source: e.g. Prince's Onslaught, Dirty Fireworks, Final Explosion, all the god-form and fusion rushes not built; Pui Pui (*Planet Zoon*), Kaioken x20, the Father-Son Kamehameha and the Final Battle against Jiren exist as missions; there is no Future Trunks Saga story chapter and no Vegito Blue; the SSJ3 Dragon Fist's dragon is particles, not a model; Sword of Hope is a sword rush with no energy blade.
2. **Verified unchanged:** Raditz Rush and Nappa Rush (`goku.js`: ids, base / Kaioken, `freeUnlock`, `freeOnly`, `perch` engine) — their tests pass (below). The Majin form: `storyLocked`, given by *The Mark* (`buu_majin`, `formGivenBy`), not on the transform button outside the story.
3. **Decision — Majin Vegeta outside the story (brief B6):** the form stays off the transform button (its place in the form list is after Blue: opening it would make Blue → Majin the next transformation; reordering moves form indices that `maxForm` rules and saved starting forms use). Free Battle / Training reach it through **Starting Form: Majin** (since the follow-up above: listed once *The Mark* is cleared). The move is earned by *The Mark*.
4. **Engine — an aim step in a rush** (`fighter.js`): `hits[i].aim: { color, hand }` — no blow; he holds the step's pose for its frames while ki gathers in that hand (a growing glow every second frame, motes drawn in), `kiCharge` once; fires the new cine event `aim`. A `ki` step's new `pierce: <m>` draws the shot on through the target (two streaks out of his back). The schema knows `aim` and reports an aim step with no blow after it.
5. **Camera** (`battle.js`): new `aimShot` (cine shot `aim`) — on the side of the raised hand, a little ahead of him: his face, the glowing hand, the target beyond.
6. **Final Impact** (`vegeta.js`, Vegeta's first `extras` entry) — a game-original name, labelled as such in the data; not Final Flash, not Final Explosion. Majin form only; earned for Free Battle / Training by clearing *The Mark* (`freeUnlock: 'buu_majin'`); `freeOnly`; 50 ki, not an ultimate. Sequence: hunched wind-up (`v_majinHeavyW`), head-first charge (`v_majinFly`), grab 120, knee into the stomach 800 (new `v_majinKnee`, `hitBody`), the hand raised at his chest for 28 frames while the ki gathers (new `v_finalImpactAim`, no blow), the point-blank shot 1900 (new `v_finalImpact`, `ki` with `pierce: 6`, `explode: 1`, `hitHeavy`, thrown away). Camera: `track`, `wide` on the knee, `aim`, a 0.8 s `impact` with a little shake; no slow motion on the blast (it would hold the brightest frames).
7. **Shared fix — the skill wheel's pointing** (`radial.js` `pick`): some lower-left slots could never be selected by keyboard, stick or mouse (an `atan2` angle −π..π against slot angles 0..2π, the gap folded only once, so a gap over a full turn came out negative and won). A five-entry wheel could not reach slot 3 (found because that is Final Impact's slot on Majin Vegeta's wheel), six entries slot 4, eight entries slots 5–6. One line.

### Checks

All run alone (scratch one-suite runner with `test` / `eq` / `ok` as in `tests/run-tests.js`): `moveset-tests` 24/24 on the final source (+4: every slot of a 2–12 entry wheel can be pointed at, and down-left on five entries is slot 3; Final Impact's id, gates (unearned / base form / story) and the Majin form still off the transform button after *The Mark*; landed against Goku and Kid Gohan — the hand held up ≥ 20 frames with no blow and the target held, ≥ 8 glows, the streak through him, reactions `hitBody hitBody hitHeavy:ki`, its five poses in order, thrown away, shots `rushTrackShot wideShot aimShot impact`, back to `free`, camera released 60 frames later; missed — camera back at once, never caught; interrupted with the hand raised, and the target getting loose there — the gathering stops, no shot after it, nobody held, camera back. Schema: an aim step with nothing after it is reported). Narrow suites on the same final source: `feel` 18/18, `immersion` 15/15, `frieza-fair` 12/12, `rof-goku` 8/8, `buu-b` 16/16, `buu-c` 19/19. Browser (port 5175, Training, Majin Vegeta vs an idle Goku and an idle Kid Gohan, `SL.Dev.unlockAll` in the page — no save touched): the wheel lists Galick Gun · Big Bang Attack · Final Flash · Final Impact · Power Down; before the fix pointing down-left selected Power Down, after it **Final Impact was chosen by holding V and pointing down-left with the keyboard and the move ran**; paused and stepped: the aim shot (his face, the glowing hand at the doubled-over Goku), the blast (Goku thrown away, the burst and ring); at the end `free`, camera back, target free, no flashes or streaks left; `SL.dataProblems` 0; no console errors. `graphify update .` run.

### Gaps / for the owner

- **Not human-tested; not seen at full speed by Claude** — the browser pane ran the battle at about 4 ticks a second while checked, so all the looks above are paused, hand-stepped frames (poses lag in those). Damage is a first guess.
- **The blast's peak frames (about three ticks) wash the frame white**: the shared hit light (`vfx.impact` level ≥ 3 adds a strong point light) plus the explosion's light. Not patched per move — the shared bright-flash task (plan 8.13).

- After the move he is left in the air where the rush took him (as after Raditz Rush) — the engine's rush, unchanged.

## Movesets and ultimates, slice 4: Goku's Nappa Rush (a rush step that stands him on the target's head) (2026-10-16) — IMPLEMENTED · NARROW TESTS + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN TESTED

Step 3 of `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` section 7 (the owner's brief *Character-specific rush combos, Majin Vegeta, and cinematic ultimates*). One bounded slice.

### Done

1. **Engine — a rush step that moves him instead of striking** (`fighter.js` rush state): `hits[i].perch: 'head'`. No blow: he hops up (6 frames, a small arc) onto the held target's head — feet at `target.pos.y + target.height`, the same x / z — plays the step's pose and stays there for its `frames`; the target stays where he is (no longer pulled in front of him), and the steps after it are struck from up there. The step's `hitPose` is played on the target as he goes up (the hit reactions lean the head away from the crown; `stand` straightens him under the feet). After the last blow (or if the target gets loose while he is up there) he springs back off and comes down to the height he went up from — the ground (landing through `_physics`), or that height when they were fighting in the air — and is not `free` until he is down (one second at most). A new cine event `perch`; the schema knows it and reports a `perch` that is not `'head'` or is the last step.
2. **Camera** (`battle.js`): new `perchShot` (cine shot `perch`) — low beside the pair, a little ahead of him, looking up so both are in frame. `wideShot`'s impact variant now takes the attacker's facing when one is straight above the other (it fell back to the world x axis).
3. **Nappa Rush** (`goku.js`, extra `nappaRush`) — game-original, after the fight with Nappa, labelled as such in the data. Base and Kaioken only; earned for Free Battle / Training by clearing *Nappa Attacks* (`freeUnlock: 'saiyan_nappa'`); `freeOnly`; 45 ki, not an ultimate. Four new poses: a low wide ready stance (`g_nappaW`), a ducked slip-in (`g_nappaPass`, the dash), standing on the head with arms folded looking down (`g_nappaPerch`), the heel stamped down off the crown (`g_nappaStomp`). Sequence: grab 120 (`hitBody`), elbow 450 (`hitBody`), cross 450 (`hitLight`), head-stand 30 frames (target `stand`), stamp 1400 (`smash`, `hitHeavy`: driven into the ground — crater and bounce). Camera: `track` alongside on the way in, `wide` on both as it lands, `perch` on the head-stand, a 0.7 s `impact` with a little shake, then gameplay.

### Checks

All run alone (scratch one-suite runner with `test` / `eq` / `ok` as in `tests/run-tests.js`) on the final source: `moveset-tests` 20/20 (+3: its own id, gates (unearned / Kaioken / Super Saiyan / story) and no target = nothing happens and no ki spent; landed against Nappa and Kid Gohan — feet within 0.05 of the crown on every held frame (measured 0.000), the target held under him the whole time and straightened up, reactions `hitBody hitBody hitLight hitHeavy`, its six poses in order, smashed into the ground, shots `rushTrackShot wideShot perchShot impact`, back to `free` and grounded, camera released 60 frames later; missed — camera back at once, never caught; interrupted on the head — camera back, target let go, Goku no longer pinned; target loose while he is up there — he springs off, lands, recovers. Schema test: a head-stand as the last step is reported); `feel` 18/18, `immersion` 15/15, `rof-goku` 8/8, `frieza-fair` 12/12. Browser (port 5175, Training, Goku vs an idle Nappa, then an idle Kid Gohan, battle paused and stepped by hand, `SL.Dev.unlockAll` in the page — no save touched): the wheel (… Raditz Rush · Nappa Rush · Dragon Fist [Needs Super Saiyan 3] …); the stance with the move's banner on the normal camera; the track shot on the way in; the head-stand from the low side shot — on Nappa's crown, arms folded (and on Kid Gohan's head); the impact shot as he springs off the stamp; back on the ground, gameplay camera, 5 hits 2,179 damage on Nappa; `SL.dataProblems` 0; no console errors. `graphify update .` run.

Found while checking: the first head-stand in the browser floated beside Nappa's head — his `hitLight` reaction leans the head back about 0.4 m from where the simulation puts the crown. Fixed with the step's `hitPose: 'stand'` (above); the screenshot after the fix shows the feet on the crown.

### Gaps / for the owner

- **Not human-tested.** Damage is a first guess. Poses in hand-stepped frames lag the state (the animator blends per render), so the owner's eye is needed on the four new poses at full speed — especially the slip-in and the stamp.
- Skipped (optional in the brief): an evade-around pass before the grab (going round him, the moves' `teleport: { to: 'behind' }` idea); the dash slips in low instead. Add when the owner wants it.
- Interrupted while on the head (only possible from a second enemy or a shot already in the air — the held target cannot act), Goku takes the hit with the engine's normal hit physics: a flier stays airborne as after any hit in the air, a fighter who cannot fly falls.
- The stamp's impact-4 hit lights the ground very bright up close (the shared hit pipeline, already open from slice 3).
- One browser `_trySpecial` call right after starting the battle returned false; the same call a moment later worked and every later one did. Not reproduced headless; not investigated (likely no target assigned yet on that tick).

## Movesets and ultimates, slice 3: integration audit, CPU camera fix, Goku's Raditz Rush (2026-10-16) — IMPLEMENTED · NARROW TESTS + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN TESTED

The owner's brief *Character-specific rush combos, Majin Vegeta, and cinematic ultimates* (pasted 2026-10-16). One bounded slice, as the brief allows: its steps 1 (audit) and 2 (the Raditz-inspired rush). Steps 3–7 are planned in `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` section 7.

### Audit of slices 1–2 (what was checked, what was found)

- Players start locked on (`Fighter.lockMode = 'locked'`), so the rule that a rush needs a target holds in normal play; a free-aiming player still gets a rush that does nothing until they lock on (pre-existing, every rush).
- **Fixed — a CPU's ordinary technique took the player's camera.** A CPU Vegeta throwing Big Bang Attacks at the player, or a CPU Goku's Instant Kamehameha, played their cine shots every time. Now a cine plays when the player uses the move, and for an ultimate aimed at the player (as the ultimates' charge shot always did) — `_trySpecial`, `fighter.js`.
- Unchanged and still open: the CPU does not use wheel-only extras (Dragon Fist, Ultimate Dragon Fist, Raditz Rush); the built-in types ignore `cooldown`; impact-4/5 hits light the ground very bright up close (seen again on the Raditz Rush kick — the shared hit pipeline, not the move).
- Audio: `SL.Audio.play` counts voices against `MAX_VOICES` 40 and frees them on a real-time timer. Five Raditz Rushes stepped back to back reached 24 counted voices, back to 0 four seconds later, the context `running` — no leak from these moves. A burst of more than 40 sounds inside a second or so still drops the rest (first come, first served); the brief's audio task should start there.

### Done

1. **Rush blows choose the reaction they land with** (`h.hitPose`, `fighter.js` rush state; schema checks it is a shared pose). Before, every rush blow played `hitBody`.
2. **Wheel wording for base-body moves:** a move limited to the base body (and its techniques) says "Not in Super Saiyan" in a higher form; higher-form moves still say "Needs Super Saiyan 3".
3. **Raditz Rush** (`goku.js`, extra `raditzRush`) — a game-original rush after Goku's fight with Raditz, labelled as such in the data, not a canon technique name. Base and Kaioken only; earned for Free Battle / Training by clearing *Saiyan Brothers* (`freeUnlock: 'saiyan_brothers'`); `freeOnly` (the story's fight keeps its kit). 45 ki, not an ultimate (no armour). Four new poses — a forward-driving stance (`g_raditzW`), a shoulder-first rush (`g_raditzDash`), a clinch knee (`g_raditzKnee`), a jumping side kick (`g_raditzKick`) — with his existing cross and elbow between: knee 700 (target doubles up, `hitBody`), cross 450 (`hitSideR`), elbow 500 (`hitLight`), side kick 1300 (`hitHeavy`, knocked away). Camera: the normal battle camera for the stance, `track` alongside on the way in, `wide` two-shot through the blows, a short `impact` with a little shake on the kick.

### Checks

All run alone on the final source: `moveset-tests` 17/17 (+4: CPU camera rule; Raditz Rush id / unlock / forms / story; landed — reactions `hitBody hitBody hitSideR hitLight hitHeavy`, its six poses in order, the kick sends him away, shots `rushTrackShot wideShot impact`, back to `free`, camera released; missed and interrupted — camera back at once, nobody left held); `feel` 18/18, `immersion` 15/15, `rof-goku` 8/8, `buu-kid` 11/11, `cell-games` 22/22, `frieza-fair` 12/12, `playtest3` 15/15, `super-broly` 12/12, `universe6` 8/8, `whatif` 17/17. Failing, with evidence they predate this work: `playtest2` 25/2 — the same two failures, word for word, as a scratch copy of the pre-pass-4 backup (one of them, `a17Shield`, is documented as known in the test itself); `playtest4` 32/2 — both assert on static scene data (`goku_super_saiyan` lines) no slice touched; `--fast` 50/1 — the `char_cooler` loader gap recorded in STATUS before these slices. Browser (port 5175, Training, Goku vs an idle Vegeta, battle paused and stepped by hand): the wheel before using it (Kamehameha · Instant Transmission · Spirit Bomb · God Bind [Needs divine ki] · Raditz Rush · Dragon Fist [Needs Super Saiyan 3]); the stance on the normal camera with the move's banner; the two-shot as the knee lands (Vegeta doubled over); the impact shot on the kick (Vegeta thrown back, 5 hits, 2,889 damage); five rushes in a row with audio still running; `SL.dataProblems` 0; no console errors. Not inspected in the browser: the missed and interrupted cases (headless tests only), Kaioken, the wheel in a higher form.

### Gaps / for the owner

- **Not human-tested.** Damage is a first guess (no balance pass, per the brief).
