## Movesets and ultimates, slice 2: Vegeta's Big Bang Attack and Final Flash cinematics (2026-10-16) — IMPLEMENTED · NARROW TESTS + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN TESTED

The next step of `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` on slice 1's foundation (`spec.cine`). Outside the story only, like slice 1.

### Done

1. **Thrown techniques take part in cinematics** (`fighter.js`): `specialThrow` fires the `release` event when the ball leaves the hand (the ball kept as `sd.ball`) and is one of `CINE_STATES`, so an interrupted throw ends its shot at once. No change for a technique without `cine`.
2. **Three new shots** (`battle.js`): `handShot` (low in front, up at a one-handed pose, moving in on the hand), `frontShot` (square in front, centred, closing in), `pathShot` (cut in behind the shoulder along the thrown ball's path, holding on where it bursts). Cine shot names `hand`, `front`, `path`, and `orbit` (the existing transformation orbit).
3. **Big Bang Attack** (`vegeta.js`): `hand` during the 14-frame wind-up, `path` for 0.9 s once thrown.
4. **Final Flash** (`vegeta.js`): `orbit` as his arms spread and it gathers, `front` from frame 46 as the hands meet, `wide` (both in frame, from the side) with a little shake for 1.3 s as the beam crosses.

### Checks

All run alone on the final source: `moveset-tests` 13/13 (+3: Big Bang Attack's shots and that the ball still lands, Final Flash's shots, an interrupted throw or beam ends its shot; schema check of Vegeta); `feel` 18/18, `immersion` 15/15, `rof-goku` 8/8, `buu-kid` 11/11, `cell-games` 22/22, `frieza-fair` 12/12, `playtest3` 15/15, `super-broly` 12/12, `universe6` 8/8, `whatif` 17/17. Unchanged pre-existing failures (see slice 1): `playtest2` 25/2, `playtest4` 32/2, `--fast` 50/1. Browser (port 5175, battle paused, frames stepped by hand): the hand shot (low, up at Vegeta, the sphere in his hand), the path shot (behind his shoulder, the ball heading out), Final Flash's front shot (arms spread, the charge gathering) and wide shot (him firing on the left, the beam crossing to the impact); `SL.dataProblems` 0; no console errors. `graphify update .` run.

### Gaps / for the owner

- **Not human-tested.** The shots play on every Big Bang Attack outside the story (it is a Special, used often): 0.3 s + 0.9 s. If it feels like too much in a fight, the cine can go on the Final Flash only, or Settings → Cinematic: reduced shortens all of them.
- The PDF's "fully charged Final Flash as its own tier" is not done: this Final Flash always fires at full charge (`chargeMin` = `chargeMax`), so there is no partial tier yet.
- Final Explosion (Majin Vegeta) is not part of this step (its own story sequence, per the PDF).

**Files:** `src/game/fighter.js`, `src/game/battle.js`, `src/data/characters/vegeta.js`, `tests/moveset-tests.js` (+3 tests, +1 schema check); STATUS, HANDOFF, `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md`.


## Movesets and ultimates, slice 1: form techniques on the wheel, gates for extra techniques, technique cinematics, Dragon Fist (2026-10-16) — IMPLEMENTED · NARROW TESTS + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN TESTED

Section 4 of `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md` (step 2 of *saiyan legacy records and ultimates (1).pdf*), approved by the owner with three answers: SSJ4 Goku stays the separate GT character; main Goku's Dragon Fist unlocks after `buu_kidbuu`; the Spirit Bomb stays on the Ultimate button in every form.

### Done

1. **Bug fixed — the skill wheel showed the base form's techniques** (`radial.js`): it read `def.specials`, now `f.specFor(slot)` like the HUD cards. Visible before: Final Form Frieza's wheel said "Death Beam Volley" for his Death Ball.
2. **Shared gates for extra techniques** (`Fighter.extraLock`, used by the wheel and `_trySpecial`): `forms: [...]` ("Needs Super Saiyan 3"), `freeUnlock: '<mission>'` (hidden until cleared — the forms' rule), `freeOnly` (hidden in the story), and `ultimate: true` now obeys the battle's allowUltimates and counts as an ultimate. A technique's own wording still wins on the wheel (God Bind: "Needs divine ki"); God Bind keeps its own form check because Broly's mission hands it over on Special 2.
3. **Technique cinematics** (`spec.cine = { steps }`, fighter.js `CINE_STATES` / `CINE_SHOTS`): steps fire on the technique's events — `start`, `release`, `connect`, `finish` — with `at` frames, `shot`, `dur`, `sfx`, `quake`, `flash`, `slow`, `mood`. Shots are gameplay shots (Settings → Cinematic off / reduced still apply), only when the player is in it, **only outside the story** (story set pieces keep their own camera — the Spirit Bomb missions are untouched). Safety exit: back to `free`, the last shot finishes; anything else (hit, knockdown, clash, scene) or a missed rush ends the shot at once. New shots in `battle.js`: `riseShot`, `rushTrackShot`, `reverseShot`, `wideShot` (wide two-shot, or held on the point of impact with the lens tipping up). Schema reports unknown cine events / shots and unknown `forms`.
4. **Goku** (`goku.js`): Spirit Bomb cine (low behind him tilting up to the sphere, the whole arena at 2.6 s if still charging, then the follow shot); Instant Kamehameha snaps in behind the target; **Dragon Fist** — a new wheel extra, SSJ3 only, `freeUnlock: 'buu_kidbuu'`, `freeOnly`, 100 ki (its only limit — see the cooldown finding below), an ultimate rush with its own three poses (`g_dragonFistW`, `g_dragonFistDash`, `g_dragonFist`), two blows (1200 + 4800), a gold trail coiling round him on the way in and a gold coil rising out of the last blow (`vfx.dragonTrail`, `vfx.dragonCoil`); it can be dodged or guarded and the camera comes back for the punish.
5. **SSJ4 Goku** (`gt_cast.js`): **Ultimate Dragon Fist** — its own wheel ultimate (`g4UltDragon`), three blows, the last driving him up (new pose `g4_dragonFinish`, held as the finish), a dragon half again the size, its own cine (upper body, track, two-shot on contact, impact). The Dragon Fist button (`g4Dragon`) stays and now shows the gold trail.

### Checks

All run alone on the final source, except that the very last edit (dropping two `cooldown` fields that never applied, see below) was followed by `moveset-tests` only: `moveset-tests` 10/10 (new); `feel` 18/18, `immersion` 15/15, `rof-goku` 8/8, `buu-kid` 11/11, `cell-games` 22/22, `frieza-fair` 12/12, `playtest3` 15/15, `super-broly` 12/12, `universe6` 8/8, `whatif` 17/17 (the first three also ran before the change: same counts). `playtest2` 25/2 and `playtest4` 32/2 and `--fast` 50/1 — all pre-existing: playtest2's two (the Saibamen pack's "shook it off as the player", `a17Shield` barrier damage 0 — the test's own comment calls this one known) fail identically on a scratch copy of the pre-pass-4 backup; playtest4's two assert on static scene data (`goku_super_saiyan` line order and wording) this change does not touch; `--fast` is the known `char_cooler` loader gap. Browser (port 5175, frames stepped by hand with the battle paused): SSJ3 Goku's Dragon Fist stance / track / impact shots, the gold trail and coil, SSJ4's Ultimate Dragon Fist (own banner, wheel: Kamehameha · Dragon Fist · 10x Kamehameha · Ultimate Dragon Fist), the Spirit Bomb rise shot; `SL.dataProblems` 0; no console errors. `graphify update .` run.

### Gaps / for the owner

- **Not human-tested.** Damage numbers are first guesses (the PDF: balance later, with evidence).
- The CPU does not use the new extras (Dragon Fist, Ultimate Dragon Fist) — only hand-coded AI uses extras today.
- A rush needs a locked target (as every rush already did): in free aim the wheel offers Dragon Fist but it does nothing until you lock on.
- The dragon is particles (a coil of gold motes), not a modelled dragon.
- **Found, not changed:** any impact-5 blow lights the wasteland's ground almost white for a moment when the camera is close (a plain heavy hit does it too); the impact shot makes it more visible. A shared hit-light tune-down would touch all combat — the owner's call.
- **Found, not changed:** the built-in technique types (`beam`, `projectile`, `rush`, `spiritBomb`) never apply a spec's `cooldown` — only teleports and registered techniques do — so the `cooldown` written on many rushes (SSJ4 Goku's Dragon Fist: 150) does nothing. Applying it would change every such move's balance; left for the owner (the new moves carry no cooldown).

**Files:** `src/game/radial.js`, `src/game/fighter.js`, `src/game/battle.js`, `src/game/techniques.js` (comment), `src/render/vfx.js`, `src/data/schema.js`, `src/data/characters/goku.js`, `src/data/characters/gt_cast.js`, new `tests/moveset-tests.js` (not yet wired into `sim-tests.js`); `planning/MOVESET_ULTIMATES_PLAN.md`, STATUS, HANDOFF.


## Records screen scrolls (2026-10-16) — IMPLEMENTED · BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN TESTED

Step 1 of the owner's *saiyan legacy records and ultimates (1).pdf* ("Records tab -- fix the scrolling").

**The bug:** the Records screen (`SL.Screens.records`, `src/ui/screens_menu.js`) put every panel straight into the screen, which does not scroll (`html, body, #app` are `overflow: hidden`). With every mission in the Overall list and every fighter in the Fighters list the content was 5872 px tall in a 768 px window, so most of it and the **Back** button were off screen; the player had to zoom the browser out.

**Done:**
1. The panels and the Sound Test row now sit in one `.scroll` box between the title and **Back**; the title and Back stay where they are (`src/ui/screens_menu.js`, screen class `records`).
2. `.records .scroll` (`css/game.css`): fills the space left, scrolls vertically, thin orange scrollbar that is always visible when there is more to see. The Gallery and Lore panels keep their own 62vh scroll (the wheel scrolls them first, then the page).
3. Keyboard / gamepad: up and down scroll the records by 40 % of the box; once at the end they move between Sound Test and Back as before.

**Checks (Claude, browser on port 5175, 1024 × 768, normal zoom):** title at 46–94 px, scroll box 112–648 px, Back at 674–722 px — all on screen; mouse wheel over the panels scrolled the box by 500 px; 25 presses of down reached the bottom, one up went back; with the Sound Test row shown (in-memory flag, restored) it was visible at the bottom, focus moved to Back on the next down; no console errors. No automated test covers the Records screen; full suite not run (UI-only change).

**Files:** `src/ui/screens_menu.js`, `css/game.css`; STATUS, HANDOFF.


## Android 18's customization: the Buu Saga look, her era looks in the story, a fuller build (2026-10-16) — IMPLEMENTED · NARROW TESTS + VIEWER + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN (OWNER'S INSTRUCTION) · NOT HUMAN TESTED

Built on the wardrobe foundation of the session below (no new system). **Backup:** none by Claude (the owner makes their own, per the brief). Source manifest at the end (same method as below): `0324c8ce9988c9c4f952b4177ad5cef2adcdf13d1b5dfbc9325407b7ec2fe589`.

**The owner's choices (2026-10-16, asked before building):** build the **Buu Saga look** only; the reference is page 5 of *saiyan legacy next prompt.pdf* (a full-body front and back drawing: a cropped dark leather vest with a gold badge over a white T-shirt, a pearl necklace, small gold hoops, dark gloves, a gold bangle, a brown belt with a silver buckle and a gold wallet chain, teal capri jeans with rolled cuffs, orange slouch socks, black flat shoes; a side-parted bob tucked behind the left ear); the Super era's story wears that casual look, and the Tournament of Power keeps the tracksuit; and a fuller figure ("thicky ... good hamstrings and other parts").

### Done

1. **`a18_buu` — Buu Saga** (`src/data/wardrobe.js`): an outfit on `a18_classic` (`own`: her authored look with changes — the stripes and the skirt removed, the short-sleeved tee, a short open vest, gloves, belt, capris, socks, flats), era `buu`, unlocked by clearing **"bog_party"** (her first mission after the Buu Saga). It brings its hair (`hair: a18_buu`) and its **props**: 44 pearls laid on her chest's surface (measured in the viewer), two hoops, the badge, the bangle, the wallet chain.
2. **Hair `a18_buu`** (`src/data/hair.js`): her bob, parted on her right; the fringe swept across to her left, where the side is tucked behind the ear (the hoop shows); her right side and the back hang straight and are **cut blunt** (`shape: 'cyl'` locks — square ends, not points). The Android Saga look keeps the `trunks` bob.
3. **Story by era** (`profiles.android_18.canonByEra`): `android` → `a18_classic`, `buu` → `a18_buu`, `super` → `a18_buu`. Checked in real headless missions: "The Pair on the Road" wears the Android Saga look, Bulma's party (`bog_party`) the Buu Saga look, `top_jiren` the tracksuit. **The Tournament of Power missions' `appearance` overrides are still needed** (the Super era's look is now the Buu Saga one) — kept.
4. **A fuller build** (`androids_pair.js`, every look and Free Battle): narrower waist (0.145 → 0.122) over wider, deeper hips (pelvis 0.168 × 0.134), heavier shaped thighs (`thighR` 0.096, `thighShape` with the hamstrings raised: `ham` 0.16), firmer calves, a fuller seat (`build.seat`). Bust unchanged (0.75). **Future 18 inherits it** (her appearance copies 18's).
5. **Engine, opt-in (defaults unchanged):** `SL.Wardrobe.appearance` adds an outfit's `props` to the character's own; a `null` in an `own` outfit's look now removes an authored field (it silently did not: `outfitLook` lays the look over a `start`) — `src/game/wardrobe.js`. `src/game/model.js`: `outfit.crop` (capri trousers ending partway down the shin in a rolled cuff), `build.seat` (two soft forms on the back of the pelvis in the trousers' colour), `outfit.gloveLen` (glove cuff length, default 0.55), `outfit.socksH` (sock height, default 0.07).
6. **Tracksuit** `a18_top`: `bustSoft: true` (no ink ring round the small bust forms, which read as bumps).

Tried and rejected: `bustShape` (her vests and jacket are shells over the plain chest: the shaped bust pierced them); bust 0.9 (the bust forms poked through the Buu vest).

- **Browser (Claude, port 5175, its own storage):** Customization → Android 18: the three outfits, "3 of 3 outfits owned", Buu Saga's corner "Story look · MAJIN BUU, SUPER"; a Free Battle mirror match — the player in the Buu Saga look, the opponent auto-dressed in the tracksuit, the HUD portrait in the new look, combat paused and rendered close; no console errors. 18's saved look on 5175 restored to default afterwards.

### Gaps / for the owner

## System polish before Android 18's customization: Free Battle looks, Customization hints, outfit-aware intros, a voice and an entrance for every fighter, aura-coloured afterimages (2026-10-16) — IMPLEMENTED · NARROW TESTS + HEADLESS ROSTER SWEEP + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN (OWNER'S INSTRUCTION) · NOT HUMAN TESTED

The owner's brief: before Android 18's outfits, audit and improve the shared customization foundation (outfits, slots, hair, auras, transformations, unlocks, story-locked vs Free Battle looks, saves), character presentation (intro, idle, flight, transformation, victory) and small high-impact polish; implement what is safe and useful; leave 18's own outfits for a later session.

**Backup:** made by the owner before the session (not made or verified by Claude). Source manifest at the end (`sha256sum` over the sorted `src tests tools vendor css assets index.html` file list): `ce184d3af6dfbb4196785490c1148000f4abfaa4785e7a505c27d6c5f5169386`.

### Audit (what exists; what was missing)

- **Customization foundation — works, kept.** `SL.Wardrobe` (data `src/data/wardrobe.js`): 14 profiles (Goku, Vegeta, Bardock, Future Trunks, adult Gohan, Videl, Gogeta, Caulifla, Tien, Android 18, Kefla, Kid Gohan, young Chi-Chi; the dev mannequin); slots outfit / armor / emblem / scouter / tail / hair, palettes, Kefla's body controls, young/adult bodies, outfit-borne `hair` and `face`, `own` outfits (the authored look plus changes — the route 18's outfits will take), `.glb` data. Story missions never read the player's choice; What-If only with *My look*; Free Battle / Training / menus wear the saved look, sanitized; saves in `v3` with `migrate` on load. Nothing here needed rebuilding for 18.
- **Gaps found:** the Free Battle fighter cards always showed the default look, and changing the Outfit row did not redraw the card; the opponent always wore their saved look, so a mirror match was two identical fighters; the Customization rows hid what was still locked (a locked scouter or hair simply was not there); nothing said which eras' story wears an outfit.
- **Aura selection:** only the preview's On / Off exists. Every form's aura is canon identity (MASTER_SPEC §15 allows alternates "only where appropriate") — **not added**; it needs the owner's design call (which characters, which forms, Free Battle only).
- **Transformations × outfits:** a form's hair (`form.look.hair`) replaces an outfit's own hair — Future Trunks' shoulder-length Cell-Saga hair becomes the standard Super Saiyan hair; Kid Goku's young body takes the adult forms' hair styles. **Not changed** (GT shows a young Goku in Super Saiyan; owner's call whether to add per-outfit form hair).
- **Presentation:** 131 playable fighters; **110 had no Free Battle intro line and 117 no victory line** (silent); Krillin, Tien, Yamcha, Cui, Mecha Frieza and Android 16 opened with the plain combat stance (16 also won in it); Vegeta's intro tapped a scouter he was not wearing (the old Known limitation); Future Trunks' *Jacket, No Sword* look reached for and sheathed a sword he does not carry; 63 fighters without `vfx.ghost` left pale-blue afterimages whatever their aura.
- **Harness finding (pre-existing, not changed):** `node tests/run-tests.js --fast` = **50 passed, 1 failed** — "unlock rules reference real characters, stages and missions" fails on `char_cooler` because the fast runner's hard-coded file list (`tests/run-tests.js` line 307) predates the roster expansion (`cooler.js` and the other newer character files are not loaded). Identical result on the pre-pass-4 backup. The fast schema check therefore does not cover those characters; the headless loader (`tests/headless.js`, from `index.html`) does, and `SL.dataProblems` is empty there.

### Done

1. **Free Battle / Training setup** (`src/ui/screens_select.js`, `src/main.js`, `src/game/wardrobe.js`): the fighter cards show the look each fighter will wear and redraw when the Outfit row changes; a new **Opponent Outfit** row (Dummy Outfit in Training) picks the opponent's outfit for that battle only — nothing is saved; **Auto** is their saved look, and in a **mirror match** the opponent wears the next outfit the player owns (Kefla, with one outfit, the next colours) so the two read apart. `SL.Wardrobe.opponentLook(charId, outfit, playerId)`; `SL.App._opponentLook` passes it as the CPU fighter's `wardrobe`. The Outfit row also keeps Kefla's body values when the outfit changes.
2. **Customization screen** (`src/ui/screens_wardrobe.js`, `css/game.css`): every slot row says how many pieces the story still holds back ("1 to unlock"; the Outfit row after its era); the title corner names the eras whose story dresses the character in that outfit ("Story look · Androids / Cell") and how many outfits are owned ("8 of 9 outfits owned").
3. **Outfit-aware presentation steps** (`src/game/animator.js`, `src/game/model.js`, `src/data/schema.js`): a `sequences` step may carry `needs: 'scouter'` or a prop id (`'!sword'` when it must be absent) and is skipped when the worn look lacks it (`Model.wears`). Vegeta's intro taps the scouter only when he wears one (`vegeta.js`); Future Trunks without the sword stands calm instead of reaching for a hilt and does not sheathe after a win (`future_trunks.js`). The schema validates the field.
4. **Intros of their own** for the six that opened in the plain stance, from poses they already own (no new animation): Krillin raises the Destructo Disc hand, Tien the Tri-Beam triangle, Yamcha the Wolf Fang stance (`z_fighters.js`), Cui the mocking point (`namek_force.js`), Mecha Frieza his boast (`cold_force.js`), Android 16 stands with no guard and wins looking off into the distance (`androids_pair.js`). Expressions unchanged, so portraits are unchanged.
5. **A voice for every fighter** (`src/data/dialogue.js`): 227 new lines — an `<id>_intro` and an `<id>_victory` for every playable fighter that lacked one (Free Battle's intro and win; story missions do not use these keys). Paraphrased English written for this project, one voice per character (Kid Buu, Hirudegarn, Bandages and the like in cries; Magetta in steam).
6. **Afterimages in the fighter's aura colour** (`src/game/fighter.js` `ghostColor`): a fighter without `vfx.ghost` of their own now leaves afterimages in the current form's aura colour (Broly's Legendary green, Goku Black's purple, SSJ4 red, Hit's violet); fighters with their own colour are unchanged.

### Checks (on the final source unless noted)

- `tests/wardrobe-tests.js` alone: **25/25** (2 new — the opponent outfit: Auto, mirror outfit, mirror colours, chosen, locked refused, a real Free Battle wearing it, nothing saved; the step conditions: the tap and the draw with scouter and sword, skipped without them, the schema clean). The Bardock screen test first failed on the new counter's word "unlocked" (its "no internal message" check matches `locked`); the counter was reworded to "owned" — the assertion was not changed.
- `tests/character-pass-tests.js` alone: **10/10** (1 new — every playable fighter has an intro line, a victory line and an intro that is not the plain stance; a real Free Battle intro, Krillin vs Android 16, says both lines in the new poses).
- `tests/trunks-arrival-tests.js` alone: **13/13**.
- `node tests/run-tests.js --fast`: 50/1, the pre-existing `char_cooler` failure above (same on the backup).
- Headless roster sweep (scratch script): all **131** playable fighters through a Free Battle intro (not skipped) and a win — each says an intro line and a victory line, **no warnings (no missing pose), no errors**. Afterimage colours read headless (Goku unchanged `bfe6ff`; Broly LSSJ `9aff3a`; SSJ4 Goku `ff3a5a`).
- **Browser (Claude, port 5175 with its own storage — the owner's save untouched; story progress granted through the save API):** Free Battle setup rows (Outfit, Opponent Outfit "Auto") and the cards (1280 wide: player Vegeta in the Capsule Corp armour, the mirror opponent in the Buu suit); the mirror battle's HUD portraits in the two looks; Krillin vs Android 16 intro (Krillin's raised hand, 16 standing, subtitle "Target confirmed. Engaging.") and 16's win ("It is finished. Listen... the birds are coming back." in `lookAround`); the Customization screen for Vegeta (row notes, story look, owned count) and Goku (the duplicate "Saiyan Saga, Saiyan Saga" seen there was fixed — two eras share that label). Console: no errors.
- Not checked: the full suite (owner's instruction); afterimages in the browser (headless colour only); the Tien / Yamcha / Cui / Mecha Frieza intros were looked at in a training scene, not in a real intro shot; the Free Battle cards at narrow widths are hidden by the existing layout.

### For the owner

## Chi-Chi's two looks: the child's helmet armour rebuilt, the 23rd World Tournament dress (2026-10-16) — IMPLEMENTED · VIEWER + NARROW CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN (OWNER'S INSTRUCTION) · NOT HUMAN TESTED

**The owner's correction:** the board p.194 shows TWO designs — LEFT the 23rd World Martial Arts Tournament Chi-Chi (blue sleeveless dress, red sash, white lower legs, pink shoes, long straight hair with bangs, older), RIGHT the child (pink helmet with its curved blade, blue outfit, pink gloves and boots). They stay separate; the child keeps the agreed covered version of the armour; the old model was not good enough.

**Delivered:** young Chi-Chi (`young_chichi`) now has a wardrobe with two outfits, each made for its own body, so neither can end up on the other: *Helmet Armour (Child)* `cc_kid` (default; canon look of the `early` era) and *23rd World Tournament* `cc_tournament23` (canon look of `tenkaichi23`). Catalogue → *Quality levels* → Chi-Chi (young) lists what each look has. No third costume kept: the old stylized look was the same helmet armour, replaced by the rebuilt one.
- **The child, rebuilt:** child proportions (Gotenks' approach: the build shrinks, `height` — the fighter's play scale — stays 0.84); the helmet a pink `shell` dome tipped back off the brow with its silver rim, the round stone with a blue centre on the rim and the blade a real crescent (new prop `crescent`) from the brow over the top to the back; new `hair.chichi_kid` (blunt bangs under the rim, long straight hair down the back, a lock by each cheek); bigger eyes, small cheeks, thin brows; a blue breastplate (`armor.wrap`) edged in silver `strip`s laid on its measured surface, a silver line at the hips, long pink gloves past the elbow with a teal band, knee boots with a teal top and a blue ankle strap, the teal cape narrower and clear of the body (new `outfit.capeSize`, `capeOff`).
- **The 23rd World Tournament:** the grown body (adult limb lengths, a small bust, the same play scale), new `hair.chichi_long` (blunt bangs, long side locks to the chest, a low tail), no helmet; the blue sleeveless dress (standing collar, the red-edged opening across to her right, the skirt to the knee open at the sides with a red edge — `hem.arc` + `hem.trim`), the wide red sash, full red trousers gathered under the knee, white lower legs, pink shoes, red wristbands.
- **Wardrobe engine (small):** a profile may offer several bodies (`profile.bodies: { young, adult }`, each a set of appearance fields — height, build, hair, face, headExtras, props); the outfit's `body` picks one. Profiles without `bodies` work as before (one `profile.body`; Goku's young outfits still refuse the adult body). The validator rejects unknown body names.
- **Engine fix:** a belt's coloured buckle centre (`buckleCore`) now sits on the belt when the belt is worn low (`beltAt` / `beltFit`); it was fixed at the waist. No existing outfit combined them, so nothing else moves.

**Changed files:** `src/game/model.js` (prop `crescent`; `outfit.capeSize`; buckle-centre fix), `src/game/wardrobe.js` (`profile.bodies`, validator), `src/data/wardrobe.js` (pieces `cc_kid`, `cc_tournament23`; profile `young_chichi`), `src/data/hair.js` (`chichi_kid`, `chichi_long`), `src/data/characters/dragon_ball_cast.js` (young Chi-Chi), `tests/wardrobe-tests.js` (one new test), `documentation/ROSTER_CATALOGUE.md`. Backup before the changes: `saiyan-legacy-backup-chichi-20261016-052738` beside the project (the changed files only).

Side effect, by design: missions that open on `music('menu', 0)` (e.g. "King Kai") now keep the menu song at level 0 as written — before, the launch's stop silenced it. Changed: `src/core/music.js`. Checks: `musiccheck.js` (scratch) — 95 songs, every bar sixteen steps, every note parses, every `THEMES` / `FORM_THEMES` id exists, every `THEMES` key is a character; browser (Claude, real game, dev server, audio unlocked): fourteen Free Battles in a row each started the right song with the bars advancing and full gain (Bojack twice in a row — the retry kept playing; General Blue, Tapion, Android 18, Turles, Fangs, Lucifer, Super Perfect Cell, Tora, Salza, both Gogetas unchanged, Krillin → Goku's theme); pause / resume kept the song; rematch kept it; quit returned the menu song; a story mission start ("King Kai") traced (`stop` then the mission's own cue); no console errors. Headless tests stub the music module, so no node test covers `play`.

**Shared systems (opt-in, no other character changes):**
- `SL.Toon.addRim` `rim.fur`: short zigzag ink fur strokes laid round each mesh's own axis at real size, faded out with distance (no shimmer); `rim.ribs`: ink rings and segment shading for insect-like limbs. Both use derivatives (enabled for a WebGL1 fallback).
- `Model`: `outfit.fur = { color }` (every part in that colour is a pelt; the open jacket of that colour gets a ragged opening; ragged `G.fringe` rings under the shoulders, above the elbows and over the wristband or glove), `outfit.ribs = { color, every }`; prop shapes `spike`, `claws`, `ball`; `wings.style: 'beetle'` (wing cases with ribs and half-clear membranes).
- New hair styles `vegeta_ssj4`, `gogeta_ssj4` (`src/data/hair.js`).

**Characters:** SSJ4 Goku / Vegeta / Gogeta (fur, manes, tails to the side, Gogeta's blue wristbands); Hirudegarn rebuilt as a giant on the existing Great Ape machinery (`appearance.height` 3.3 for the giant camera, `hitZones`, `turnSpeed`, `flight: false`, `giantStomp` / `giantSweep` / `giantGrab` techniques on his buttons and as `boss` patterns with `ai.hooks: 'boss'`); Omega Shenron's spikes, gems, blue under-layer, horns and talons. Details per character: the catalogue's *Quality levels* table.

**Checks actually run** (source manifest `f2d22be6f02a3851e115a7a631a7c2d2e87b07c6c2c4b12c7674676e14541648`):
- Viewer sheets and close cameras (Claude) of all five, front / 3/4 / side / back / face; the fur tuned twice after the first look (the first pattern read as scales).
- Scratch data-integrity check over the whole roster: 0 problems. Schema (`candv`) 0 problems for the five.
- Player-slot runs (every special fires, no engine error): Goku SSJ4, Vegeta SSJ4, Gogeta SSJ4, Omega Shenron, Hirudegarn; Hirudegarn's skill-wheel Tail Sweep, Hell's Claw and the Crushing Grip each fired and landed from the player slot.
- CPU fights: Hirudegarn's CPU used stomp, sweep, grab, flame, storm and melee against Goku, Vegeta, Tapion, SSJ4 Goku and Beerus. He beats normal-difficulty Goku / Vegeta / Tapion CPUs; the backup copy did the same before this pass (Goku 70 %, Vegeta 54 %, Tapion 51 % HP left), so it is not a regression — CPU fights are not human balance.
- `playtest2` technique coverage: only the older Android 17 (`android_17_top`) barrier failure, the same as on the backup; Hirudegarn's flame, stomp and storm land at six metres. `character-pass` hair / crown 3/3; `sim-tests` schema / valid / unlock 32/32.
- Browser (Claude): a Free Battle SSJ4 Goku versus Hirudegarn launched and stepped without errors.
- **Not run:** `node tests/run-tests.js`.

### Batch 3 — the older "still needs a pass" list and the remaining small gaps

**Two engine fixes found while looking:**
- **Floating feet:** gi trousers (`pantsStyle: 'gi'`) over a shoe or an ankle boot left the lower shin undrawn, so the feet floated under the trouser legs (Master Roshi, Garlic Jr., Uub, Bora, Master Shen and every other fighter with that combination). The trouser leg now continues down to the shoe (`model.js`, baggy branch).
- **`tintAmount: 0`:** a form's `look.tintAmount: 0` fell back to 0.3 (`||`), so Perfect Cell (whose form clears Imperfect Cell's dark tint with white at 0) wore a 30 % white wash — the pastel green. Now 0 means no tint (`model.js`, `model_glb.js`); Perfect and Super Perfect Cell are the board's green. No other look used 0.

**New opt-in options:** `outfit.stripes = { color, with, every }` (striped cloth, `SL.Toon.addRim` `rim.stripes`), `outfit.beltAt` / `outfit.beltFit` (a belt worn lower and tighter than the waist). New hair style `tarble`.

**Characters:** Perfect Cell / Super Perfect Cell, Hit, Majin Buu, Android 18 / Future 18, Frieza's chest dome and final-form shells (Frost kept unchanged on purpose), Shugesh, Tarble, Dr. Wheelo, Garlic Jr., Master Roshi's feet — rows in the catalogue's *Quality levels*.

**Checks actually run** (source manifest `5cc496b7910f96d66bf112e54666aa28b168bc8fade39b766ca9568869cde67a`): viewer sheets / close cameras of each (Cell's imperfect form re-checked: no plates, cream belly); data-integrity 0 problems; schema 0 problems for the 13 touched fighters; `sim-tests` subset (schema / valid / unlock / Cell / Frieza / forms) 105/105; `buu-c-tests` tearing subset 3/3; `character-pass-tests.js` 9/9; player-slot runs of Frieza, Cell and Majin Buu (no engine errors). **Not run:** `node tests/run-tests.js`.

### Batch 4 — gameplay audit, two engine fixes, Mr. See-Through

**Ki and cooldowns (engine fix, `src/game/techniques.js`):** `groundSlam` and `eruption` started from a special slot paid no ki and set no cooldown (their `start` ignored the slot), so 19 specials across the roster were free and could be repeated at once — the audit caught Magetta starting his 100-ki Molten Ground 36 times in four minutes. Both now pay through the existing `payFromSlot` (boss patterns and direct calls stay free, as the giant techniques already did).

**AI:** Magetta now uses Magma Spit (it never fired: the generic score only looked past eight metres); Tagoma's Playing Dead is a situational trick (hurt, target close), its cooldown 6 → 12 s. Both hooks do nothing in the story, where those techniques are `freeOnly`.

**Mr. See-Through (131 playable):** `Mechanics.invisible` + `Model.setCloak` — the invisible fighter the catalogue said the rig could not show. Readable by design: a glassy shimmer with a glinting edge, his shadow and dust at his feet, half-seen through every wind-up, fully seen for a moment when hit or when a blow lands or is blocked; targeting, collision and hit detection unchanged. Unlocks with the other extras after Chapter 1.

**Checks actually run** (source manifest `c7616cbe19608411c4cdca997a7cfb278406e8351cc0e69e0fb8ad24b82fe91a`): the audit (`audit.js`) over Magetta, Roshi, Tagoma, Cooler, Turles, Janemba, Gogeta, Goku Black, Hirudegarn, Omega Shenron, Metal Cooler (table in the catalogue); `sim-tests` subset (Zarbon, Nappa, Dodoria, Frieza, slams, eruptions, Magetta; includes the Frieza fairness pass and Universe 6) 71/71 after the slam fix; `rof-tests` Tagoma subset 2/2; Mr. See-Through: schema 0 problems, data-integrity 0, player-slot run (every special fires), technique coverage single cases (Unseen Barrage, Invisible Onslaught land at six metres), cloak measured over a CPU fight (`cloak.js`: a hit on him reveals him 34 times of 36), viewer at full cloak, `buu-b` every-playable-unlocks 1/1, `feel-tests` skill wheel 2/2. **Not run:** `node tests/run-tests.js`.

Four original sequencer pieces in `src/core/music.js` (`SOUNDTRACK WAVE 5`): `zamasu_theme` (the soundtrack PDF's "Justice Orchestra" entry, blocked until Zamasu existed), `ssj4_theme`, `cooler_theme`, `phantom_monster` (Hirudegarn); `THEMES` maps Zamasu / Fused Zamasu / Goku Black, the three SSJ4 fighters, Cooler / Metal Cooler, Hirudegarn, and Omega Shenron (the existing `overwhelm`). `look.headband: false` (new, `model.js`): a form can take the head band off — the Z-movie Broly's legendary form has no circlet and no gem (`look.headExtras: []`). Bio-Broly's gold strands hang down instead of standing up like horns. Viewer (Claude): the legendary face and Bio-Broly's sheet.

**Checks for batches 5–6** (source manifest `ad7f879d2d6c547205910ef450b0b43fa58d80c7f1c88002a290dace472543f6`): as above; `node --check` on every changed file. **Not run:** `node tests/run-tests.js`.

### Roster — 20 new playable fighters (33 → 53 in Free Battle and Training)

**Audit (before):** 33 playable. Story-only characters that already had complete kits (own moves, three techniques, AI profile and hooks, intro/victory/defeat poses, forms, mechanics) but were not selectable: Frieza (six forms), Cell (three), Majin / Super / Kid Buu, Hit, Androids 16/17/18, Zarbon (two forms), Frost (two), Nappa, the five Ginyu Force, Dodoria, King Cold, Mecha Frieza; also Dyspo, Android 19/20, Janemba, Super Janemba, Future Gohan, Nail, Cui, Roshi, Magetta, Saibaman, Cell Junior, Evil Buu and the absorbed Buus (story bodies). Alternate looks (Android 17's Tournament self, the absorbed Buus) were not counted.

**Made playable (each smoke-tested in the PLAYER slot — an AI moves it while the script presses transform / special 1 / special 2 / ultimate — and as CPU):** Nappa, Dodoria, Zarbon (+ monster form), Guldo, Recoome, Burter, Jeice, Captain Ginyu, Frieza (all six forms), Mecha Frieza, King Cold, Android 17 (the Android Saga's), Android 18, Android 16, Cell (Imperfect / Semi-Perfect / Perfect), Majin Buu, Super Buu, Kid Buu, Frost (+ final form), Hit. Their story fights are unchanged; each unlocks with the mission that ends his part of the story (`src/data/unlocks.js`: e.g. Frieza ← "Five Minutes", Cell ← "Father-Son Kamehameha", Hit ← "The Unfinished Match"/"The Contract"). A save that already cleared the mission gets them on the next load without an announcement.

**What had to be built to make them real fighters, not menu entries:**
- **Transformations a player can buy:** Frieza's second/third/final and Cell's semi-perfect/perfect had no ki cost (the story's boss phases change them), so a player could never transform: now 30/30/35 and 40/45 ki, each with a short `practiced` change (the story's own long sequences stay for `boss: true` changes). `form.storyBoss` (new, `fighter.js`) keeps those shapes the story's in story mode — no button buys them there, the player's or his own AI's (`controllers.js` skips a story-owned locked form, so story Frieza/Cell behave exactly as before).
- **Forms that open in Free Battle:** a `storyLocked` form that names a `freeUnlock` now opens outside the story once that mission is cleared (`fighter.js`): Frieza 100% ← "The One He Made", Golden Frieza ← "Blue and Gold"/"The Birth of Gogeta", Frost's final form ← "The Gentleman".
- **Missing techniques:** Hit had one — now **Time Cage** (special 2: the target held in stopped time while Hit's ki pays, `godBind` in violet) and **Flash Fist Crush** (ultimate: a blur of jabs from the pockets, the last under the ribs, `rush`); Super Buu had no ultimate — now the **Human Extinction Attack** (sixteen pink shots that rise and come down, `scatter`). Hit and Frost got their card text (style, ratings, blurb).
- **Copies kept as they were:** the absorbed Buus copy Super Buu wholesale — they stay story-only and Buuccolo keeps Super Buu's two old techniques (his story fight unchanged); Android 17's Tournament self stays story-only.

**Character select stays usable at 53 (`src/ui/screens_select.js`, `css/game.css`):** Select (Enter / click) on the Fighter or Opponent row opens a **portrait grid** of every fighter in story order (eleven columns; arrows move in the grid, Select picks, Back closes; locked fighters are silhouettes; the caption gives name and era); the row's ◀ ▶ still step one by one. Portraits fill in a few per frame and are cached, so the first open does not stall.

**Gameplay identity:** each kept its own story kit — Frieza's form-by-form movesets, Cell's absorption shapes, the Buus' Majin body, Hit's time-skip and patience, 16's armour and poise, 17's barrier/field and 18's Deadly Dance, Zarbon's elegance→monster, the Ginyu Force's roles (Guldo's time freeze, Recoome's poses, Burter's speed, Jeice's retreat volley, Ginyu's volley), Nappa's poise, King Cold's slow weight. Balance: they keep their boss health (30–42k), as Dabura, Broly and Jiren already do in Free Battle.

### Visual pass (Goku and Beerus as the benchmark; both intentionally unchanged)

**Benchmark qualities (what was matched, not copied):** a silhouette that reads from every side, a head sized to the body, a face with its own structure (rimmed eyes, brow, mouth that shows), clothes as separate shapes (collar, belt, plates, boots), and colours that stay where they belong across forms.

**Inspection method:** a front-view lineup of all 53 playable fighters plus the changed story cast in `tools/viewer.html`, and a full **sheet** (front, three-quarter, side, back, face front and three-quarter in one frame — a new viewer view, `view=sheet`) for ~25 of them, every form of the changed ones.

| Classification | Characters |
|---|---|
| Benchmark | Goku, Beerus (left unchanged) |
| Good, small improvements | Piccolo, Kid/Teen/Adult Gohan, Future Trunks, Bardock, Raditz, Broly, Jiren, Gogeta, Vegito, Videl, Caulifla, Kefla, Kale, Cabba, Toppo, Dabura, Gotenks, Goten, Kid Trunks, Great Saiyaman, Uub, Pan, Krillin, Tien, Yamcha, Chiaotzu, Yajirobe, Pikkon, Nappa, the Ginyu Force, Dodoria, Zarbon, Cell (all forms), Majin/Super/Kid Buu, Hit, Frost, Mecha Frieza — left unchanged this pass |
| Needed a visual pass — **done** | **Frieza**, **Vegeta**, **Android 17**, **Android 18**, **Android 16**, **King Cold**; Mecha Frieza and Frost got Frieza's face/dome fixes |
| Still needs a pass (not done) | Hit (his skull ridges read as ears), Perfect Cell (no chest/ab plating), Majin Buu (vest), Android 18's striped shirt, Frieza's chest plate (still the armour's breastplate shape) |

**Changes:**
- **Frieza:** his final, 100% and Golden forms wore the **first form's armour trousers** (purple to the knee — a purple swimsuit look). Now white legs (`look.swap`: a form swaps one authored colour — new in `model.js`), purple **forearm and shin plates** (decals drawn only in those forms — `decal.forms`, new), so Golden Frieza is gold with purple head, chest, shoulders, forearms and shins. His **face** is his own: black-rimmed slanted eyes, thin dark brow ridges, dark lips, a narrower chin, and a mouth that now shows (it sat inside his larger skull). His dome (`hair.frieza_shell`, new; Mecha Frieza, Frost and King Cold use it) sits higher on the forehead and off the skull — the old one let the white skull show through its front edge as **ragged white flames** (and white brows poked out of it).
- **Vegeta:** the hairline was a flat helmet edge on his brows; now the high forehead and the widow's peak — in every form (Super Saiyan, 2, God, Blue share the cap; Majin keeps its own).
- **Android 17 (Android Saga):** wore Yamcha's short hair and a white gi. Now straight black hair to the shoulders (`hair.a17`, new), the orange scarf, a black T-shirt over white long sleeves, jeans and belt, green trainers, a smaller head. His Tournament self shares the hair; its scarf is now visible.
- **Android 18:** had 17's shoulders and arms and a plain dark gi. Now her own build (narrower, the bust) and the Android Saga clothes: open denim waistcoat and denim skirt over a dark top, black leggings, brown boots (the tracksuit outfit is unchanged).
- **Android 16:** a soldier's little crest and a round friendly face → a low broad red crest (`hair.a16`, new), blue eyes under heavy brows, a square jaw, a smaller head on the big body.
- **King Cold:** the same dome artefact and white brows; Frieza's face, older and broader.
- Forms checked after the change and back to base: Frieza (all six), Vegeta (base/SSJ/Blue), Frost (both), Androids, Cell (base/perfect).

## Gohan versus Cell — the Cell Games made non-graphic, and the brief's missing beats added (2026-10-14, owner's choice "Non-graphic" + cinematic brief sections 35–43) — IMPLEMENTED · TARGETED CHECKS + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN (OWNER'S INSTRUCTION) · NOT HUMAN PLAYTESTED

**The owner's decision:** the Cell Games chain had been built on 2026-10-01 from an earlier brief with on-camera kills; the new brief asks for non-graphic staging. Asked, the owner chose **non-graphic**: every event keeps its order and weight, the decisive blow lands on camera, and the transition happens at the impact — light, flashes, dust, cuts to faces, silence — with no body parts, no crushing, no peeling. Same backup as the Namek pass (`saiyan-legacy-pre-cinematic-overhaul-20261014-024333`, verified).

**Delivered:**
- `cell_rampage` — the seven Cell Junior kills stay staged one by one, in the same order and variety (the chop, the rising kick, the double strike, the ankle slam, the point-blank flash, the last one caught above the ground), and Gohan's speed is unchanged; each now ends at the decisive blow in a burst of light (new CellKit burst `energy`) — no split bodies, no fragments. After the rampage the others get up off the stone (Piccolo, Krillin, Trunks: disbelief instead of desperation) before Cell's "All seven... in under a minute". Android 18 is forced out of Cell in a burst of green light (no retching, no close-up at the mouth).
- `cell_gohan` — new staged opening of the JUNIORS fight (`gh_juniors_go`): the seven split up, one after each fighter — Krillin backing away, Piccolo taking it on his forearm, Vegeta and Trunks pressed at once, Goku slow to answer — Gohan's eyes going from friend to friend with his fists closing, and Cell watching Gohan rather than the fight ("Are you just going to stand there and watch?"); then the fight is played as before. 16's end: one short controlled flash seen wide, the faces of Gohan, Piccolo and Krillin, and his head thrown clear to Mr. Satan (no body pieces, no oil). Cell's boot: the camera is on Gohan's face when it lands (a flash at the edge of the frame, one dull sound, dust); the head is hidden, never shown crushed. The Super Saiyan 2 build now has the brief's beats before the roar — the head down and the fists closing (close on the hand), the face turning from grief to anger, the dust lifting and the gold coming up unevenly with the first blue lightning — and after the change the whole silhouette is held from low. The reveal now has every reaction the brief lists: Cell surprised, Goku, Vegeta, Piccolo, Trunks, Mr. Satan's crowd, Cell interested ("Yes... YES!"), then Cell cautious (a half step back, the guard up), then Gohan.
- `cell_fatherson` — Gohan's broken arm without blood or bone sounds (the arm, then his face). **The struggle's camera follows the brief's plan:** the wide side-on shot is home and, between the story beats, it cuts away briefly in order — Gohan straining, Cell pushing, over Gohan's shoulder, the reverse over Cell's, the whole arena lit, Vegeta watching — never a continuous spin; the existing voice / Z Fighters / Vegeta beats keep their own shots. **Cell's end:** from far off, the column of light reaching him and swallowing him and the space round him (he goes white inside it and is gone in a flash), the beam leaving the sky, then a held silence over the empty arena — the armour-flaking / skin-peeling / core sequence is gone. Aftermath: Gohan on his feet, the gold leaving his hair, then Krillin ("He did it... Gohan actually did it...") and Piccolo ("...Well done, kid.") getting up, before he falls forward smiling and Vegeta's moment.
- `cell_games` (Goku's Instant Transmission Kamehameha, same chain): the point-blank beam swallows Cell and a column of green vapour hangs where he stood (no half body left standing); he regrows inside it as gathering green light and steps out whole.

**Kept as it was (working, and not graphic):** Gohan's base and Super Saiyan fights, Cell's hug, 16's last words, the red-line/gold-tears transformation design, the TOYED phase, the swell, Goku's farewell and King Kai's planet, Trunks struck down by the thin beam (he falls; nothing shown), the Father–Son Kamehameha's gameplay (Cell's beam first, one-handed answer, Goku's voice, the Z Fighters, Vegeta's blast, the player's final push).

**Checks actually run (final source manifest `245c1f075ab0b1f00b4d1d6686abd1081478b9e7a422cb21d4fefeb5bd8b470d`):** `tests/cell-games-tests.js` run alone through a scratch runner — **22/22** (two tests updated to the owner's new non-graphic choice and made stricter: the boot lands on Gohan's face, no bone-crack sounds, no crushed head, no body pieces in 16's scenes; the executions contain no split bodies or fragments and every one ends in light; the IT Kamehameha leaves Cell hidden in vapour, not cut); unskipped headless runs of `cell_gohan`, `cell_rampage`, `cell_fatherson` with every line logged in order; bot runs (repository brains) `cell_games` seed 7, `cell_gohan` seed 5 and from the JUNIORS checkpoint, `cell_rampage` seed 5 and from RAMPAGE, `cell_goodbye` — all success, data problems 0; a headless shot log confirming the struggle inserts fire during a clash. Browser (Claude, frame-stepped + numeric framing): the Juniors split-up, the SSJ2 build shots (dust and gold rising, the held silhouette), Cell's wide engulf shot, the aftermath reactions. **Not run:** `node tests/run-tests.js` or any full suite. **No human playtest.** Note: my own simple bot cannot win the Father–Son clash (it does not press the struggle input); the repository's chain test does and passes — this was the same before the change.

**Still incomplete:** pacing of the new beats needs a human; Final Flash (`cell_absorb`: Cell's shoulder and purple blood) and the future Androids (`cell_future`: bodies in pieces) are outside the Cell Games and still graphic — they belong to the brief's Priority 4 / section 44 and need the same treatment when those are reworked; the HUD stays up during staged beats; Priorities 2–5 of the brief are not started.

## Goku versus Frieza on Namek — cinematic restoration pass (2026-10-14, owner's "Complete cinematic restoration" brief Priority 1 + "Namek saga large text" brief) — IMPLEMENTED · TARGETED CHECKS + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN (OWNER'S INSTRUCTION) · NOT HUMAN PLAYTESTED

**Backup first (the brief's most important rule):** complete copy of the project folder at `C:\Users\NemOK\Documents\coding\saiyan-legacy-pre-cinematic-overhaul-20261014-024333` (outside the project, local only, nothing uploaded), with `BACKUP_MANIFEST.tsv` inside it (relative path, size, SHA-256 of every file). Verified against the originals after the copy: **1235 files, 116,583,997 bytes, 0 missing, 0 extra, 0 mismatched.** No project file had been changed before the backup was verified.

**The spine the owner asked for is kept in order:** Kaioken x20 Kamehameha fails → Spirit Bomb seems to end it → Frieza survives → Piccolo takes the beam → Krillin → the first Super Saiyan → Frieza at 100% on the dying planet → saucers → mercy → betrayal → escape. No mission order, save structure, checkpoint index or story outcome was changed (Frieza leaves Namek alive for Chapter 5, as before).

**Delivered, mission by mission:**
- `namek_goku` — OPEN rebuilt as scene `goku_frieza_confrontation`: wide and slightly above (green sky over the top half, the two far apart), Frieza on a low rock (`stage.addSpire`), Goku on the low ground in his guard; the two short original lines ("You've hurt enough people..." / "You still believe this battle is something you can control."); Frieza leans in and lifts off, the rock gives under him, Goku drops his weight. BASE now opens with the staged first exchanges (`goku_tests_frieza`): Goku advances in bursts, the first punch passes (Frieza side-steps), the second is blocked on a forearm, a short counter (shown from Frieza's side); Frieza's string — blocked, slipped, dodged — a change of direction and a clean hit that sends Goku sliding back on his boots with a dust trail, camera at ground level, Goku looking up at Frieza hovering. Then the player has control. New scene `frieza_shows_the_gap` before the Kaioken: eyes narrow, aura, a rush the camera only just keeps, a blow from the front, one from the side, Goku through a rock pillar (debris, crater), he gets up; "You keep standing up. I will give you that." / "I'm still here." / "...I've been holding back since the moment you arrived."; Gohan leans in, Krillin can't take it in. `goku_kaioken_escalation`: the Kaioken form and its red aura are on him at the burst (they were only applied after the scene), low-angle burst shot, and the first Kaioken rush (punch and kick land, a burst behind Frieza, Frieza turns and counters).
- `namek_kaioken20` — the x20 Kamehameha charge is the brief's seven shots (face; the hands coming together, aimed at the real palm joints, the energy ball between them; the stance; low angle with the red aura; the wide shot with Frieza waiting; Frieza's face as he feels it climb; back to Goku as it fires). The beam is tracked from low and to the side with a guaranteed two-shot, and halfway through the push the camera goes behind Frieza holding it on his palm. `frieza_deflects_kamehameha`: a burst of light drives Frieza back first ("it looks like it worked"), then the deflection; the red fades off Goku, shoulders down: "That was... everything I had." / "Then you have very little left to offer." before his legs go.
- `namek_spirit_bomb` — Goku's quiet line after the impact no longer repeats the x20 line; `frieza_returns`: everyone sees him before anyone speaks, then quiet first ("You nearly convinced me that I could lose.", camera closing on his eyes), then the rage.
- `namek_super_saiyan` — `krillin_taken`: Krillin comes toward Goku to help; a shot holding Goku on the ground and Krillin up in the air (the distance between them); Goku starts forward, too far away; the flash seen from Goku's position, then his face with the sound dropped out (tinnitus). `goku_super_saiyan` rebuilt on the brief's nine shots: head lowered → the clenched fist → the face (almost a whisper: "I tried to end this without letting it take anything else from us.") → low angle as the first energy rises (stones lifting round his boots) → dust going out and the sky going dark → the change (wide) → the gold burst pulled back (flash 0.5, the silhouette stays visible) → his eyes → a low hero shot → reactions (Gohan, amazement and grief; Piccolo, serious; Frieza's fury turning to disbelief) → only then Goku sends Gohan away with Piccolo (moved after the change, as in the story) → Goku facing Frieza over his shoulder → Frieza's anger. New staged exchanges in the reversal: `ssj_first_exchange` (Frieza rushes; Goku moves at the last moment and answers with one short punch to the body; Frieza is driven back while Goku has not moved; Frieza's string — blocked, slipped, the third countered; "How... how is he this fast?") and, halfway through the reversal, `ssj_sky_exchange` (up into the sky against the green, an attack from above intercepted arm on arm with a shockwave, the counter flinging them apart, a pause apart at different heights, Frieza landing heavily, Goku several metres away).
- `namek_end` — `frieza_full_power`: the change shown full-body (the silhouette broadening), a low angle as it finishes with "One hundred percent. You wanted to see everything I could become — look carefully.", then Goku composed. **100% costs him:** his rest between bursts grows the longer he holds it (45 → 80 frames in the 100% fight, 85 → 140 in the fight that breaks him) and in the second fight he stops to breathe now and then (steam, the `exhausted` pose); Goku notices ("He's burning through it... He can't keep this up."). New scene `goku_its_over` before the saucers (as in the story): Frieza bent and steaming, Goku lets the aura down — "It's over. You can stop now." / "You think YOU can decide when this ends?" — he turns to go, the disc comes up over Frieza's hand behind him, and he backs off into the air where the saucer fight starts him.
- **Clothing:** the Namek gi (`goku_namek`) now has its own progressive damage (`DAMAGE_GOKU_NAMEK`: dust, then the orange split over the navy undershirt at the chest and back, then the trousers at thigh and knee): level 1 from the pillar, 2 from Kaioken x20 on, carried through the Spirit Bomb and the Super Saiyan mission; the 100% fight still tears the gi off as before, and the gi rips go with it.

**Bugs fixed (underlying logic, not hidden):**
1. **Kaioken x20 overloaded in the middle of the Kamehameha cutscene** — `strainScale = 0` ("the scene owns the body") fell back to 1 (`|| 1`), so strain built at full rate; the form collapsed with an explosion, a stagger and a "Whoa... nice hit!" bark mid-scene. `Fighter._updateStrain` now honours 0; the scene also keeps his ki (x20 drained 14/s and could power him down mid-charge).
2. **Two-shots cut a fighter out on narrower windows** — `Cinema.twoShot` assumed a 16:10 screen; it now uses the real canvas shape (headless keeps 1.6).
3. **Semantic two-shots jumped across the line mid-shot** — the side was re-chosen every frame, so the lens flipped when a fighter turned; it is now chosen once per shot.
4. **Spectators took stray blasts in `namek_goku`** — Gohan was floored by a 4,322-damage stray blast in a bot run; Krillin, Gohan and the buried Vegeta are now intangible there (as Staging's background role already makes them elsewhere).
5. Frieza appeared on the wrong side for the pillar kick, Goku's guard pose hid his face during his line, the x20 close-ups were washed out by the aura, the hands shot missed the hands, low-angle and hero shots cut the boots, the Goku-and-Krillin shot lost Goku — all found in the browser and fixed.
- Checked and **not** bugs: the earlier "namek_super_saiyan never ends" and "namek_aftermath stuck" results came from my own probe not passing the brain its phase; with the repository's `MissionBrains.attach` both missions complete.

**Checks actually run (final source manifest `5586f449f0788a98be7085918808c3acb040e711a5884a43296389f170b34df3`):** unskipped headless runs of every edited mission with every line logged in order; bot runs on the final source (repository brains, 21 runs) — `namek_goku` seeds 7/5 (plus one with every scene played) and from checkpoints 1 and 3, `namek_kaioken20` seeds 7/5 and from checkpoints 3 and 4, `namek_spirit_bomb` seed 7 and from checkpoint 1, `namek_super_saiyan` seeds 7/5 (plus one with every scene played) and from checkpoints 2 and 4, `namek_end` seeds 7/5 and from checkpoints 5 and 6, `namek_aftermath` seed 7 — all success, data problems 0; targeted test files through a scratch runner (run before a last comment-only edit): wardrobe 21/21, Frieza fairness 12/12, power gap 2/2, playtest3 Namek/Frieza/Kaioken/two-shot tests 10/10, playtest4 Namek tests 18/18, what-if Namek 2/2. Browser (Claude, frame-stepped, numeric framing checks plus screenshots): the opening, both staged exchanges, the pillar, the Kaioken burst and rush, all seven x20 shots, the behind-Frieza struggle shot, the "everything I had" beat, Krillin's approach, the POV flash, the fist, the low angle, the burst, the eyes, the hero shot, Piccolo's reaction, the SSJ first exchange, the sky exchange (rise, pause, landing), the 100% reveal, "It's over", the saucer behind Goku's back. **Not run:** `node tests/run-tests.js` or any full suite (owner's instruction). **No human playtest.**

**Still incomplete / for the owner's eye:** pacing and feel need a human (several beats are short, 0.1–0.3 s strikes); the HUD stays on screen during staged beats (as in every staged phase); King Kai and Vegeta reactions are not added — Vegeta is dead and buried at this point in the game's (and the story's) continuity, and King Kai is not represented in these missions; Piccolo is not present before the Spirit Bomb in this game's continuity, so the gap-reveal reactions are Gohan's and Krillin's; Gohan and Krillin still wear their Earth gis on Namek (canon: battle armour) — no Namek wardrobe for them exists yet; the "Frieza attacks the planet" beat stays where the game already had it (before 100%, in `namek_super_saiyan` CORE); no new music. Priorities 2–5 of the brief (Trunks, Bardock, Android/Cell, Buu/Super) and Gohan versus Cell are not started in this pass.

## Longer cutscenes — Kale's berserk change, Kefla's fusion, the finale's throw, fall and wish (2026-10-14, owner's choice "Longer cutscenes") — IMPLEMENTED · TARGETED CHECKS + BROWSER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN PLAYTESTED

**`top_saiyans` BERSERK (≈30 s, was ≈15):** the ToP refinement brief's nine steps in order — (1) close on Kale's face as her sister runs ahead ("Sis... you're so far ahead of me now"), Caulifla's SSJ2 boast; (2) the body tenses, a tremor, "...left... behind..."; (3) the surge — green lightning, stones lifting, dust driven out from her feet, an orbiting camera; (4) the hair flickers between the two looks; (5) the body **swells** — the model's scale grows over 80 frames while the camera tilts up from her boots (the aura held down so it does not swallow the shot); (6) the aura blows outward (the emerald pressure wave, a crack in the stone, Goku and Caulifla pushed back into their guards); (7) the pull-back to the scale of it; (8) Goku, Caulifla, Champa, Beerus and Whis react; (9) she charges without hesitation and Goku takes it on his guard, sliding back.
**`top_saiyans` POTARA (≈37 s):** Champa's earrings streak down; each puts one on (close on the hand at the ear; new poses `tp_earL`/`tp_earR`, the Potara prop appears on the ear); the pull together (accelerating, gold and green trails spiralling, an orbiting camera, the light growing); the burst (a 0.7 flash, not a white-out) and a silhouette in the fading light; Kefla from the boots up; she **tests her speed** — three readable dashes round Goku, each ending in a skid; Champa, Beerus, Whis, Goku react; "Call me Kefla" — and the first blow lands on his guard, his boots scraping back (the doc's Phase A), before the title.
**`top_finale`:** THROW — Goku's "...Got it!", the stands' disbelief (Krillin, Vegeta's "Do it, Kakarot"), and halfway through the flight a shot over Jiren's shoulder as he turns to meet Frieza; EDGE — mid-push Jiren's face resisting ("I... will not... be beaten!") and near the end the frontal view with the boundary beneath them (by grip, or by time if the bar is not pushed that far), a slower fall with a quiet exchange (Frieza: "don't you dare call this teamwork"; Goku: "it worked, though"), then Universe 7's stands; WISH — 17 looks down at his hands ("...Any wish at all."), a far wide shot from out in the void with Super Shenron now larger and further out so he dwarfs the platform, and the **restoration as an event**: the eyes blaze, rings of gold light roll out, the void warms, the camera sweeps the stands while the erased fighters return one by one, the Zenos ("Everybody came back!"), the Grand Priest. **Bug fixed:** the phases after the elimination re-applied Golden Frieza in the stands (`stage(m, over)`).

**Checks actually run (final manifest `1451dfda51716c83b3d3cb1e591ff5d712a2d6e9672a7e92303e581cac055972`):** every edited phase run unskipped headless (all lines in order); full bot runs `top_saiyans` seeds 5 and 7 complete (Goku ends at 8% / 1% — the 2-on-1 is close for the bot), `top_finale` seeds 5 and 7 complete, `top_kefla` seed 7 complete; browser (Claude): the swell shot (camera confirmed in front of her, aura no longer filling the frame), Caulifla's and Kale's earring close-ups, the far wide wish shot before and after enlarging the dragon, the restoration sweep along the stands, Frieza's form after the elimination; data problems 0, no console errors. **Not run:** `node tests/run-tests.js`. **No human playtest** (timing and pacing need a human eye).

**Still incomplete:** screenshots of the fusion silhouette and Jiren-turn shot were not captured (the browser pane only presented part of the canvas late in the session; the cameras were checked numerically); the HUD (health bars, skill bar, form banners) stays on screen during these staged beats as in every staged phase; no music cue changes were added for the new beats.

## Supporting cast pass — Android 18, Krillin, Tien, Piccolo, Gohan, Master Roshi (2026-10-14, owner's choice "Supporting cast") — IMPLEMENTED · TARGETED CHECKS + BROWSER/VIEWER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN PLAYTESTED

**Delivered:**
- **Android 18** (`androids_pair.js`, brief §26): her own guard (weight on one hip, hands loose), a left straight, a spinning back elbow, a high roundhouse with the other leg, an axe heel, a one-handed shot; Deadly Dance remapped to them; shorter recoveries (quick to recover after a blocked blow); AI hook `android_18` — punishes a whiffed strike at once, and with her own back to the drop she circles toward the middle instead of trading there. Android 17 (Android Saga) unchanged.
- **Krillin** (`z_fighters.js`, §30): compact straights from a low crouch, a shin-height snap kick, a low spinning kick, a palm thrust; evasive AI (higher defence, short bursts).

- **Piccolo** (§23): patience — short bursts and long watchful rests at his range (his kit and ally-protection were already his own). **Gohan** (§24): team support — the AI goes after whoever is on a reeling ally (`AIHooks.adult_gohan`, Piccolo's).
- **Master Roshi** (new story cast, `top_cast.js`, §29): short, bald, dark glasses with green frames, white beard and moustache, white vest, khaki trousers (the owner's stand stills). Old master's low guard; a deliberate palm, a back-fist off a drunken sway, a leg sweep, a guard-breaking turtle palm; Kamehameha; **Thunder Shock Surprise** (in close, a grab with everything behind it); **Max Power** form (the body swells: bulk, muscle, scale; harder to stop, slower) with the Max Power Kamehameha. - **Tournament looks** (`wardrobe.js`): `tien_top` — the dark vest with the red 天, cream sleeves, white sash, dark red trousers; `a18_top` — the pink tracksuit zipped to the collar, white trainers. Wardrobe profiles for Tien and 18 (`tien_classic`, `a18_classic` = their authored looks), and every `top_*` mission dresses them (mission appearance). Saiyan-saga Tien is unchanged.

## Remaining forms pass — Goku's Super Saiyan, Cabba's Super Saiyan, God Toppo, Frost, Magetta (2026-10-14, owner's choice "Remaining forms"; Vegeta's suit stays charcoal by the owner's answer) — IMPLEMENTED · TARGETED CHECKS + BROWSER/VIEWER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN · NOT HUMAN PLAYTESTED

**Engine (small, data-driven):**
- `src/game/fighter.js`: `form.practiced` — every transformation after the story's first plays a short, controlled sequence; a story's own moment (`boss: true`: the Namek awakening, `C.transform` ascensions) keeps `form.sequence`. Sequence steps gain `dust` (dust driven out from the feet) and `small` (a burst sized like a quick one, so the glow does not swallow the silhouette). `move.quake { r, trauma, color }` — a blow that comes down into the ground in front of him (crack, dust, ring, camera tremor). `def.footfall { every, vol, pitch, dust, trauma }` — a heavy body's steps.
- `src/data/schema.js`: `practiced` is validated like `sequence`.
- `src/game/controllers.js`: AI hooks `magetta` (never backs off; with the target near the drop and himself on the inside he walks in and shoves; the body press at middle range) and `frost` (the hidden needle as a cheap shot when you commit or turtle, not as an opener).

**Characters:**
- **Goku — Super Saiyan** (brief §5): `practiced` (1.7 s): braces, fists tighten, the camera comes in, a small disturbance and dust from his feet, the aura grows, gold, the camera pulls back on the silhouette; no lightning or darkened sky (the Namek awakening keeps its own). Own AI (presses, then reads; between base and God/Blue).
- **Cabba — Super Saiyan** (§32): own strike timing (a frame sooner, the drill kept), martial AI with measured rests, his own practiced change.
- **Toppo — God of Destruction** (§38): his own strikes — a frame later, armour behind every light blow, harder push; the hammer drives violet destruction into the stone (`quake`). (No new special: the mission's Hakai spheres already make the pressure zones.)
- **Frost** (§33, "not a recoloured Frieza"): look from the reference — white chest with one blue gem, blue shoulders, blue hands, blue knee gems. His own kit: the first form fights like a gentleman, the left hand behind his back (Beerus' verified arm), open-handed — a delayed palm (the opener waits, then lands), a backhand, a tidy knee, a spinning heel; **Hidden Needle** (special2, a tiny fast projectile that doubles you over); Frieza-race ki kept for range. Patient AI (long rests, a sudden burst). Final form: a low two-handed guard, a faster two-handed string, longer bursts.
- **Magetta** (§34): weight — a lumbering walk with footfalls (thud, dust, tremor), poise and armour on every swing (light blows do not stop him until enough of them break it, then a long stagger), whole-body haymakers, a two-handed shove (what walks you off the edge; second in his string), a hammer into the stone (`quake`), a shoulder charge; **Iron Body Press** (special1: a telegraphed jump crash, cooldown 8 s). His magma and steam stay the mission's own.

**Checks actually run (final manifest `fca8374b78f16d9994aa95ad5824cda594e119b7c804b29bd7d27d6e3b03471f`):**
- Headless: practiced vs story sequence — Goku 104 frames (gold at 53) / story 156 (gold at 101); Cabba 96 / 130. AI-vs-AI smoke fights (40–60 s, several seeds): no errors; Frost uses the palm, spin, needle (5× a minute), death beam/volley, final-form string; Magetta armours 20–34 blows, poise breaks 1–2×, haymaker/shove/hammer, body press (6× a minute before the cooldown was raised to 8 s); Cabba Super Saiyan strings, rush, beam.
- Mission bot runs: `u6_cabba` complete seed 7 (seed 3 rings out in phase 6 — the same with the new Cabba data switched off); `top_toppo` PRIDE checkpoint seeds 3/7 complete, seed 11 rings out in the Final Explosion phase — the same with God Toppo's new strikes switched off. `u6_frost_goku/piccolo/vegeta` and `u6_magetta`: the bot fails phase 1 on every seed tried **with the old kits as well** (a probe switch restored the old Frost/Magetta data) and survives as long or longer with the new ones — the bot cannot play these missions; they need a human playtest.
- Scratch clothing check 32/32 and last session's mechanics script 19/19 re-run (shared fighter/controller code changed).
- Browser (Claude): data problems 0; viewer — Frost (look, palm, backhand, needle, behind-the-back arm), Magetta (hammer wind-up and strike); in game — Goku's practiced Super Saiyan (the braced close-up; after the burst the silhouette readable once the burst was made `small`).
- **Not run:** `node tests/run-tests.js`. **No human playtest.**

**Still incomplete:** Piccolo, Krillin, Tien, Roshi, Android 18 (supporting cast) untouched; Frost's tail is not animated in his strikes; Magetta's body press uses the generic slam (no custom landing quake beyond it); the practiced Super Saiyan exists for Goku and Cabba only (Vegeta's, Gohan's, Caulifla's still play their full sequences every time).

## Tournament of Power — character, animation, combat and staging refinement pass (2026-10-13, owner's "ToP refinement" brief) — IMPLEMENTED · TARGETED CHECKS + BROWSER/VIEWER CHECKS (CLAUDE) · FULL SUITE NOT RUN (OWNER'S INSTRUCTION) · NOT HUMAN PLAYTESTED

**Starting state confirmed:** source manifest `e27efd36…` (unchanged since the previous entry). Reference images: the 15 pictures embedded in `ToP refinement.pdf` (extracted locally to the session scratchpad for viewing only; nothing bundled, nothing downloaded).

**Systems changed (shared, small):**
- `src/render/vfx.js` Aura: `style.inner` — a second, tighter aura shell so two energies stay distinct (Blue under Kaioken's red, Ultra Instinct's bright core); `style.trail` — colour streaks off the body at speed.
- `src/game/fighter.js`: god evasion takes `swayVel`/`stepVel` (Ultra Instinct moves only as far as the blow needs) and `overrun` — a slipped attacker's weight carries on through the empty space, an `overreach` pose and a recovery step (new common pose in `poses_common.js`).
- `src/game/model.js`: `look.skin` (a form recolours the skin; God of Destruction Toppo) alongside last session's `look.paint`.
- `src/game/techniques.js`: two techniques — `timeSkip` (Hit: still preparation → unseen for ~9 frames while a small violet distortion gathers at the destination → back inside the guard at the shoulder → one precise blow → back to stance; no screen flash) and `barrier` (Android 17: faint shell with a bright icosahedral outline, opens in 6 frames, blasts fizzle on it, an attacker swinging into it is pushed off, untouchable while it holds). Both registered in `src/data/schema.js`.
- `src/game/controllers.js`: AI hooks `hit` (time-skips when the target commits — mid-swing, charging, firing) and `android_17` (barrier only for an incoming blast or a close rush).
- `src/game/stage_top.js`: a red edge wash on the paving nearest the player within 8 m of the drop (off in scenes / non-ring-out stages); camera-occlusion fade for the rim pillars and outcrops (they ghost out when they stand between the camera and the player's fighter — found in the browser check: edge fights were hidden behind them).
- `src/data/missions/top_kit.js`: `TK.mood(m, mood, ids)` — eight stand reactions (excitement, concern, shock, disbelief, frustration, pride, silent, celebration), two pose variants per mood so neighbours differ, angels/Grand Priest stay composed, both Zenos react together; six generic spectator poses (`sp_*`); Dyspo's seat; ruin slabs kept low (they were tall enough to block the camera).

**Characters changed:** Goku (UI Sign: minimal slips, overrun, watching rhythm, restrained multicolour + inner core; MUI: decisive rhythm, own faster strike timing, inner white core + trail; Blue: inner core; Blue Kaioken: blue inner layer under red, red-blue trail, body tint cut from 0.22 to 0.07); Vegeta (Blue: sharper compact aura; Blue Evolution: deeper inner layer, trail, **ki drain 2.6/s**, high-pressure rhythm); Hit (reference colours: purple skin, dark armour, teal undersuit, lilac coat trim; idle with occasional foot/head/shoulder shifts; `timeSkip` special; reads-the-player AI); Kale (**new `controlled` form** separate from berserk: own hair `kale_ctrl`, pupils back, smaller body, guard-up idle `ka_ctrl`, own heavy/dash, martial profile; berserk now `berserker` personality with whiff overcommit); Caulifla (SSJ and SSJ2 each with own AI and quicker strings; SSJ2 overcommits); Kefla (SSJ and SSJ2 AI; SSJ2 quicker recoveries, longer charge); Jiren (full power: own faster light chain, dark-red inner aura, trail; base keeps the walking, waiting rhythm); Toppo (God of Destruction: red-brown hide via `look.skin`, magenta-edged unstable aura with dark inner core, ranged-pressure AI); Android 17 ToP (barrier special2, economical AI); Golden Frieza (own AI: ranged pressure, theatrical rests; gold inner aura + trail); Zeno (reference: violet bands on the head sides, magenta vest over a grey chest panel, yellow sleeves, blue hands); Grand Priest (teal outfit, orange chest mark, halo standing behind the head); **Dyspo** (new story cast with a full kit: lean rabbit-eared Pride Trooper, low sprint pose, fast short strikes, `speedOrbit` Light Speed Mach Attack, rush specials; in the stands/edges of the ToP missions, not on the select screen).
