**Face and body pass — Caulifla / Kefla (2026-10-11, latest; UNVERIFIED, automated testing still deferred; character-local only — `caulifla_kefla.js`):** measured against the rendered skull (manual raycasts): at headR 0.13 the default mouth sat 3.6 mm *inside* the skull, and `face.lips` was never read (the builder reads `face.mouthColor`). Now: `mouthColor` (dark rose), `mouthY` / `mouthZ` place the mouth 0.7–1.2 mm proud of the skin — visible in neutral, smirk (the global expression's tilt) and shout, and attached in profile. What the controls actually do: `jaw` / `jawW` move and narrow a sphere that sits *inside* the skull unless the cranium is lifted; `cheeks` < ~1.3 adds spheres fully inside the skull (no visible effect), so it stays 0. So `skullY` 0.05 lifts the cranium and a longer, narrower jaw (Caulifla 1.2 / 0.72, Kefla 1.18 / 0.78) forms a tapered lower face and chin; `skull` slightly narrower. Eyes: wider, lower almond shape (eyeW ~1.35, eyeH 0.6–0.64), spaced out (eyeX), steeper slant; heavier, lower brows. Caulifla softer and mischievous; Kefla narrower eyes, steeper slant, heavier brows. Body: Caulifla slimmer (narrower chest, waist 0.098, slimmer arms, smaller shoulder joints); Kefla athletic (broad shoulders, fuller chest depth, waist 0.104, wider hips, thighs 0.1). Bust values lowered so the two bust spheres sit inside the bandeau / top (they cannot merge into one form: they would only overlap above bust 1.28) — the chest now reads as one smooth shape. Outfits, hair, moves, stats, forms, unlocks, missions unchanged. Manual look (Claude, one session, fixed camera, aura off): front, three-quarter and profile; neutral, smirk and shout; four face close-ups and one gameplay-distance shot.

**Limitations found (checkpoint):**
- **Brow fix (APPLIED 2026-10-11, owner-approved narrow exception; UNVERIFIED by automated tests):** `src/game/model.js` `_buildHead` — `browMat.userData.baseEmissive = new THREE.Color()` (white in THREE r149, so after the first `_updateEmissive` every brow glowed white for good) is now `new THREE.Color(0, 0, 0)`. Nothing else in `model.js` changed. Manual browser check (Claude, Training): Caulifla and Kefla through a real `_tryTransform` base to Super Saiyan, then a real `takeHit` from the other fighter: brow emissive peaks during the hit flash (`afafaf`) and returns to `000000`; brows show gold `e8c040` / lime `98d03a`. Goku (existing fighter): base `000000`, after transform (his next form is Kaioken) and a hit, the brow settles at Kaioken's intended red tint `2a0401`, not white.
- No eye / brow depth control: eyes and brows sit at fixed fractions of headR, so a narrower or flatter skull makes them stand off the face (why `skull` stays near 1).
- The skull is one ellipsoid: the profile stays rounded (no flat face plane), and the jaw sphere leaves a faint shading seam round the chin.
- The chest is one fixed shape plus two bust spheres; a natural merged chest contour (and a non-cylindrical bandeau) needs new chest geometry.
- **Proportions and smile pass (2026-10-11, latest; UNVERIFIED, automated testing deferred; character-local, `caulifla_kefla.js` only):** fuller bust as chest depth (Caulifla chestD 0.138, Kefla 0.153) so chest and top stay one smooth shape; bust raised only to just under the measured show-through point (0.62 / 0.42). Hips and thighs: pelvisW up, thighR up (Caulifla 0.086 with harem.thigh 1.49 so the trousers keep their width; Kefla 0.106, hips 0.168, hipX 0.094, shins 0.063). Kefla's leggings: three soft volumes per thigh as joint decals (hamstrings, quadriceps, outer hip-to-thigh sweep) — they move with the thigh joint; the glute-to-hamstring curve shows from the side and rear. Faces: brows thinner and less slanted; `form.restFace` (existing per-form hook) gives a resting smirk to Caulifla and the existing `warm` smile to Kefla in both forms; a short painted `line` crease at the raised mouth corner makes the smile read. Rig, outfits, hair, moves, stats, forms, unlocks and missions unchanged. Manual browser look (Claude): fixed camera, fighters turned to front / three-quarter / side / rear, before and after; face close-ups base and Super Saiyan; `SL.dataProblems` empty.
  - **Limits found:** `G.limb` is a uniform capsule (no thigh taper), so thigh shape comes from added volumes — Kefla's thighs can read slightly like breeches from the front. Caulifla's loose trousers hide her hips and thighs; a seat volume under them was invisible and was dropped. The mouth is a flat box with no curve: the smile is a tilt plus the corner crease, and the crease stays as a small mark beside an open (shouting) mouth. Bust spheres still cannot merge, so bust fullness is limited to chest depth.
- **Kefla body-geometry pass (2026-10-11, latest; UNVERIFIED — automated testing deferred):** new opt-in shaped surfaces in `src/game/model.js`, default off, used only by Kefla: `shapedLathe` (a lathe whose vertices are moved in place, seam at the back, normals averaged across it), `G.chestBust(k)` from `build.bustShape` (the chest lathe's front pushed forward in two overlapping soft lobes, fuller below the centre; the bust spheres are skipped) and `G.thighShaped` from `build.thighShape` = { top, mid, knee, quad, ham } (full at the hip, tapering to knee × shinR, quadriceps and two hamstring heads raised out of the same surface; only for fitted legs — trousers with fullness keep the capsule). Kefla: chestD back to 0.145 (no whole-torso thickening), bustShape 1.4, thighR 0.1 with thighShape {1.18, 1.04, 1.1, 0.1, 0.12}; the six thigh decal ellipsoids and the bust spheres are removed. Caulifla unchanged.

Default appearance: Goku and Caulifla still build the original 225-vertex chest lathe and capsule thighs. `SL.dataProblems` empty.

Vegeta had no lasting main-story Blue. Plan source: MASTER_SPEC §3 (no detailed RoF plan) + `documentation/RESURRECTION_OF_F_VISUAL_REFERENCE.md` Reference A §4–6, Reference B §3.

**Where to play:** Story → Chapter 17 · Resurrection of F → **Three Minutes** (`rof_finale`), right after "Blue and Gold", before Chapter 18 (Cabba). Developer Menu → `rof_finale`, phases 0–6.

| Phase | What is played / shown |
|---|---|
| `confront` * (scene `rof_finale_confront`) | From Blue and Gold's last picture: Vegeta in front of the spent Golden Frieza; "Krillin! The senzu beans — one for Kakarot, one for Piccolo"; Krillin feeds Goku (he sits up, then stands, full health); Gohan feeds Piccolo, who gets up (health restored, arms crossed); Frieza sneers; "Kakarot let you live. I am not Kakarot." |
| `blue` (hold Charge) | **Vegeta's first lasting main-story Super Saiyan Blue** (`SL.data.cinematics.ascend`; skipping never answers the hold): the opening lens on the ground looking up at him with Frieza beyond (Reference A §4 Shot 1), then the face; the form is taught (`Mission.teach 'form:blue'`). |
| `duel` * (play) | **Super Saiyan Blue Vegeta vs Golden Frieza** (Frieza at 70 %, a real AI fighter, aggression 0.8): no story floor on Vegeta — **a knockout is a defeat** (retry from here). Ends on Frieza's story floor (15 %). Power Down disabled for the duel. Optional: 12 hits. |
| `fall` (staged) | The gold goes out of Frieza (form → final, Blue and Gold's Golden recolour undone); on his knees in a crater; **over Vegeta's shoulder, the palm raised and charging** (Shot 2); Frieza's face to the dirt, his palm driven into the ground (Shot 3): the field cracks and glows red, bolts, quake; **Whis raises his barrier round Goku, Gohan, Piccolo, Krillin and Tien**; Vegeta is outside it ("VEGETA! Get over here!"); the white-out. |

Local to this mission: the stage is rebuilt, nothing is saved. |
| `finish` * (cue + staged) | Three minutes earlier: Vegeta's palm over the kneeling Frieza (final form) — **Goku, already Blue, leaps over Vegeta: "FRIEZAAA!"** (Reference A §6 Shot 1); **the player fires the God Kamehameha (cue: Ultimate)** — side-on beam, Frieza to dust before his hand reaches the ground (Shot 2). The cue goes ahead by itself after 8 s if untouched (the project's no-one-stuck rule); a skipped leap does not answer it. |
| `after` (scene `rof_finale_after`) | Vegeta furious at the stolen kill; Goku and Krillin explain the rewind; Goku's offer to train together, scoffed at (Shot 3); Whis' last word; the closing title names the next mission. |

## Chapter 17: Resurrection of F — "Blue and Gold" (Goku vs Frieza) (2026-10-11, newest) — FUNCTIONAL PROTOTYPE · TARGETED CHECKS 8/8 · BROWSER-CHECKED BY CODEX · HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED

**Implemented scope:** the next encounter after `rof_invasion`, confirmed from the current handoff, MASTER_SPEC §3 and source before implementation. The invasion was already built; its latest full baseline was **816/0** on `0f7f1b144686e74d`, newer than the earlier 808/1 report. MASTER_SPEC approves Golden Frieza, Blue and Vegeta's major role; HANDOFF supplies this encounter's sequence. No separate approved RoF planning document was found. This mission covers Goku's turn and the handover to Vegeta; the saga is not complete.

**Campaign access:** Story → **Chapter 17 · Resurrection of F → Blue and Gold** (`rof_goku`), after **The Thousand Soldiers**. Fresh-save Universe 6 access now waits for this encounter; already-cleared Cabba / End of Z saves retain access. Universe 6 stays Chapter 18 and End of Z Chapter 19. `u6_cabba.js` is byte-for-byte unchanged. Cabba's scripted Blue flash still grants no lasting Vegeta main-story form.

**Forms and continuity:** Goku's canonical Blue giver and Frieza's canonical Golden boundary are now `rof_goku`. A matching-movie override retains `bb_legend` / `bb_fusion` as the Broly campaign's own reveals, independently of canonical completion; the movie remains separate. Free Battle Goku Blue opens after either `rof_goku` or `bb_legend`. Vegeta's existing Blue giver and Free Battle requirement remain `bb_wrath`; his lasting canonical Blue reveal is still missing. This encounter locks all non-base Vegeta forms and uses only base / Blue Goku. The real held-Charge reveal teaches Blue; merely skipping or idling at the prompt does not teach it.

| Phase | Play / visible story beat |
|---|---|
| `arrival` * | Goku, Vegeta and Whis descend into the invasion field; Piccolo remains down, Gohan grieves; Frieza challenges Goku and Whis warns against carelessness. |
| `base` * | Base Goku vs Final Form Frieza. Player damage must push Frieza to his story floor; Goku's knockout is a real defeat. |
| `full` * | Visible Frieza 100% transformation, then a second playable base-Goku fight with real defeat. |
| `blue` | Hold Charge for Goku's Super Saiyan Blue reveal using the existing ascension sequence. |
| `golden` | Visible Golden Frieza reveal using the existing transformation sequence. Mission-local gold body recoloring keeps the purple accents; movie materials are untouched. |
| `duel` * | Super Saiyan Blue Goku vs Golden Frieza. Power Down is unavailable during this story stretch, including in the radial menu. |
| `stamina` * | Frieza visibly tires; his ki and damage decrease and vulnerability grows. Player attacks must still finish the duel: waiting cannot win automatically. |
| `ambush` | Goku lowers his guard and returns to base; Sorbet visibly aims and fires, the beam hits Goku, who collapses injured. |
| `after` | Base Vegeta moves between Frieza and injured Goku; Krillin assists Goku. Ends on Vegeta taking the field, before his duel or Blue reveal. |

\* checkpoint. Developer Menu: `rof_goku`, phases **0 arrival, 1 base, 2 full, 3 blue, 4 golden, 5 duel, 6 stamina, 7 ambush, 8 after**. Knockout in any of the four fights fails the mission; retry restores that fight's controller, forms, health, targets and cast. Watched / skipped cinematics restore the same relevant outcome. Tuning: `SL.data.rofGoku.TU` in `src/data/missions/rof_goku.js`.

**Reuse / integration:** existing Goku / Frieza / Vegeta / Whis and invasion cast, Whis outfits, plains stage, combat AI, `Mission.storyFight`, checkpoints, `cinematics.ascend` / `transform`, Cinema / Staging, buuKit and brolyKit. New mission and three scenes in `src/data/missions/rof_goku.js` and `src/data/scenes/rof_goku.js`. Integration is limited to story/load lists, the existing film brain, canonical vs matching-movie form grants, Free Battle's alternate unlock, and the radial menu honoring the existing reversion limit. No save-schema, movie mission, Cabba mission or general combat refactor.

**Verification:** new `tests/rof-goku-tests.js` **8/8** on the final encounter source: campaign access and old saves; four fight losses / retries; actual held Charge and idle / skip safety; Blue / Golden checkpoint state; Free Battle alternate giver; canonical / What-If / movie chronology isolation; watched / skipped ambush and handoff; full watched seed 11 and skipped seed 5 playthroughs. Existing affected modules ran **44/44** (RoF 6, Universe 6 7, Super Broly 12, Buu-c 19), and `node tests/run-tests.js --fast` **51/0** after the shared integration changes. Focused modules were invoked through a session runner using their exported functions, not claimed to execute by running their module files directly; all are registered in the normal full-suite runner.

**Full-suite checkpoint:** `node tests/run-tests.js` ran once, after checking active runs: **825 passed, 0 failed**, in **44 min 26 s** (2026-10-11, 08:25:46–09:10:13 +02:00). The source SHA256 matched before and after the run. Source snapshot SHA256: `cfb3b17463c6b56e143b5a2d8c52eec27e711d6c94ce20f37ef5cc2da27efabf` (sorted path / SHA256 manifest over `src`, `tests`, `tools`, `vendor`, `css`, `assets`, `index.html`). The older Broly coverage gap remains closed.

**Extra balance evidence:** watched seeds **3 and 7 completed**, with Goku at **63.14% / 47.90% before the scripted ambush**; seed **19 lost in the full-power fight**, with no engine errors or warnings. These extra runs preceded the final mission-local palette / guard-pose visual fixes; their gameplay tuning is unchanged. The final scripted 12% injury is not a balance measurement. Bot balance is provisional, and not every seed wins.

**Focused browser verification (Codex, separate developer save):** the real Story menu displayed the new Chapter 17 card and launched the encounter; arrival / dialogue continuity, full-power phase, Blue / Golden fight presentation, gold body with purple accents, Goku's ambush collapse, base Vegeta visibly standing between Frieza and Goku, and Krillin's assistance were inspected, using developer phase starts for later beats. Fresh-save access gates and the sustained held-Charge input were verified headless; browser automation did not complete the hold. Not every cinematic frame or full human fight was checked. Corrections during verification included the local Golden palette, scene movement / skip-state consistency and the inability to strand Goku in base during the Blue fights. No browser errors / warnings appeared in the final focused check. Owner's usual save was not used.

**Remaining gaps:** Vegeta's lasting canonical Blue reveal and duel, Earth's destruction, Whis' rewind and the definitive RoF outcome are unbuilt. Tagoma's disappearance / end and the Tagoma–Ginyu bridge are unresolved; this encounter does not supply that missing transition. Frieza's revival wish / tank, Jaco, Master Roshi and Shisami remain missing earlier content. Prototype art, fight camera readability, pacing and difficulty need human judgement. Cell Games rework and movie polish remain deferred.

**Next bounded development task:** one RoF finale encounter beginning with injured Goku / Krillin behind base Vegeta and low-health Golden Frieza alive. Follow the approved progression for Vegeta's first lasting canonical Blue, reuse the existing movie form independently, and implement his duel plus the destruction / rewind / final intervention and outcome as a recommended grouping consistent with HANDOFF's remaining milestones. MASTER_SPEC supplies broad saga requirements and no detailed RoF plan exists. Do not silently mark the missing earlier continuity beats complete.

**New owner reference material (received after source freeze):** both complete texts are saved in `documentation/RESURRECTION_OF_F_VISUAL_REFERENCE.md`. The exact ocean / orbital / macro staging, Sorbet's ring close-up and hiding rock, gi wound, Frieza's chest stomp and Goku's two-finger counter are not implemented by this prototype. The existing Whis outfits are reused, with exact details pending comparison. The film reference lists Piccolo among Whis's survivors. Existing source leaves him down after intercepting the beam, without setting a death state; establish his recovery before the finale rather than assuming a confirmed death or resurrection. These references do not expand this completed task into the entire saga.

**Universe 6 chronological backlog:** Goku vs Botamo → Goku vs Frost → Piccolo vs Frost → Vegeta vs Frost → Vegeta vs Magetta → **Vegeta vs Cabba (built)** → Vegeta vs Hit → Goku vs Hit → tournament ending. The five missing earlier rounds precede Vegeta vs Hit. Completing Cabba alone does not complete the tournament.

| 6 | Desperate Alliance (`bb_alliance`) | FUNCTIONAL PROTOTYPE | **Survive the barrage** of green spheres (24 s); Vegeta lands beside Goku and goes Blue (low, shoulder to shoulder); **duo fight with party switching** (the partner fights on as the ally AI; objective: hit him from another angle while he is busy with the partner); the synchronized rush — both caught, heads smashed, pinned, green light in his mouth — **Instant Transmission** (Special 2), "Have fun, Frieza!", pop; Frieza's trembling hand; Piccolo's plateau, the Fusion Dance, Vegeta refuses — then agrees. |
| 7 | The Birth of Gogeta (`bb_fusion`) | FUNCTIONAL PROTOTYPE | Piccolo demonstrates every step; **first dance** (rhythm: Light on the beat) — a bent finger: **Fat Veku**; **play Frieza** against Super Saiyan Broly on the lava ("the hour" on the HUD clock, 36 s of play), **Golden Frieza** by holding Charge half-way; **second dance** — half a beat late: **Skinny Veku** (new); **third dance**, judged (a miss means "again", wider window; after three tries it succeeds) — the light column — **base Gogeta**, "Call me Gogeta", Instant Transmission. |
| 8 | The Ultimate Fusion (`bb_gogeta`) | FUNCTIONAL PROTOTYPE | **A** the arrival between Frieza and Broly; the green barrage, **Weave** (Dodge ×3: low tracking, orbital, over the shoulder); Super Saiyan (boots, fists, eyes, the column from above). **B** the counter (Heavy roundhouse, Light four-hit, Blast double palm), Broly through the pillars. **C** the aerial fight; the **beam clash** (Ultimate: Big Bang Kamehameha against his mouth beam; a wide side-on lens with both fighters in it). **D** **Super Saiyan Blue** (hold Charge: the aura gold → magenta → cyan, the column from the sky, boots to face). **E** **Full Power Broly** (the arena wrecked) and the fight. **F** the fists meet — reality cracks like glass and falls away — **the Dimension** (a kaleidoscope of magenta, violet, cyan and emerald with crystals and beams at every depth). **G** the fight inside; "It's futile" (Dodge, the lean) and the inverted spin kick (Heavy); **God Punisher** (Special 1: stance, spheres, low dash, kick up, pursuit, crossed arms, barrage, detonation); **Stardust Fall** (Special 2); **Soul Punisher** (Ultimate: the rainbow orb thrown — he survives — Gogeta's smile); the heel-kick and the second fracture. **H** back on the lava: uppercut, hammer, meteor kick (Light, Heavy, Heavy), the crater. **I** terror in his eyes; the **Kamehameha** (hold Charge — KA-ME-HA-ME — HA!, Ultimate); Shenron's eyes, **Cheelai's wish**, Broly gone in a green flash, the beam on into the sky; Gogeta's smile. |
| 9 | A New Beginning (`bb_epilogue`) | FUNCTIONAL PROTOTYPE | Frieza aims at Cheelai and Lemo's escaping ship — **Stop him** (Light): Gogeta at his wrist; Frieza leaves; the fusion runs out. *Some days later*, Vampa: **play Goku**: find Broly (marker), **throw the capsule** (Light) — a Capsule Corp house and supplies; the goodbye ("call me Kakarot"), the pop, Broly's small smile, the green sky; the credits. |

### HUMAN PLAYTEST CHECKLIST — the complete film
1. **Movies screen:** ten cards in order with "Part 1…9"; only Part 1 open on a fresh save; each opens after the one before; a cleared part replays.
2. **Part 1:** does the city read as the Saiyans' home? Bardock's fight: fair, short? Is the pod launch a moment? Does the planet's end (from orbit) land, with no white-out?
3. **Part 2:** is Vampa harsh enough? Is the beast readable (the furrow, the red ring, the lunge) and fun to hit? Is the collar scene clear without being graphic?
4. **Part 3:** God Vegeta's sways and the catch — do they feel effortless? The drag and the crimson blast into the sea — does the green pillar out of the water land?
5. **Part 4:** the warm-up — every one of the twelve beats readable at normal speed? Is the God Bind clear (press Special 2), and does its breaking look like glass?
6. **Part 5:** the Blue — the emerald and silver flashes visible but brief? The melt — does it read as the glacier turning to lava, not a cut?
7. **Part 6:** party switching obvious? The flank objective understandable? The escape timing?
8. **Part 7:** the three dances — are the failures funny, and is the third one fair to pass? Playing Frieza: does Golden Frieza still feel outmatched?
9. **Part 8:** every stage A–I in order; the clash framing (both fighters visible); the dimension (readable fighters on the colours?); the God Punisher / Stardust Fall / Soul Punisher; the Kamehameha and the wish.
10. **Part 9:** the ship, the wrist; finding the camp; the goodbye's warmth; the credits.

**Exact steps:** Settings → Developer / Test on; Main menu → Developer Menu → pick the mission and a phase → Launch. Phases — `bb_planet`: 0 exile, 1 plan, 2 return, 3 gine, 4 launch, 5 fall, 6 after · `bb_vampa`: 0 exile, 1 hunt, 2 beast, 3 ba, 4 training, 5 arrival, 6 after · `bb_clash`: 0 arrive, 1 watch, 2 gold, 3 adapt, 4 god, **5 dominate**, 6 overwhelm, 7 smash, 8 after · `bb_wrath`: 0 wake, 1 rage, **2 ocean**, 3 blue, 4 fury, 5 clash, 6 end · `bb_goku`: 0 warmup, 1 base, 2 gold, 3 ssj, 4 god, 5 bind, 6 after · `bb_legend`: 0 blue, 1 duel, 2 paragus, 3 legend, 4 after · `bb_alliance`: 0 barrage, 1 together, 2 duo, 3 caught, 4 piccolo, 5 after · `bb_fusion`: 0 lesson, 1 first, 2 frieza, 3 second, 4 perfect, 5 after · `bb_gogeta`: 0 arrive, 1 counter, 2 aerial, 3 beams, 4 blue, 5 full, 6 break, 7 dimension, 8 return, 9 kame, 10 after · `bb_epilogue`: 0 ship, 1 later, 2 visit, 3 goodbye, 4 credits.

**Tuning knobs:** `SL.data.bbPlanet.PV`, `bbVampa.VP` (the beast's health), `bbClash.CL`, `bbWrath.WR`, `bbGoku.GK`, `bbLegend.LG` (melt seconds), `bbAlliance.AL` (barrage), `bbFusion.FU` (dance pass mark, Frieza's seconds), `bbGogeta.GG`; Broly's reading per form `forms[i].legendary`.

**Known limitations and risks (honest):** balance is tuned on bots only (the beast's health, the barrage, the dance pass mark, Frieza's survival) — a human may find parts too easy or too hard; the Developer Menu phase numbers of *The First Clash* and *Wrath* moved up by one after the new phases. all new art is Level 1 prototype (Gine, King Vegeta, Ba and the beast were built without close references). Many iconic moments are staged sequences answered with one button (cues), by design for the film's choreography; the free fights are Bardock's squads, the beast, the stones, Vegeta's fights, Goku base / SSJ / God, the Blue duel, the barrage, the duo, Frieza's survival and Gogeta's aerial / Full Power / dimension fights. Golden Frieza is a gold cast over the final form. In the dimension the fight is in the air (the hidden terrain still exists far below). The credits are title cards. Dialogue is original, in the characters' voices; three short phrases the owner named ("It's futile", Cheelai's wish, "call me Kakarot") are kept to a few words.

**Automated:** `tests/super-broly-tests.js` 12/12 (registry and lock chain of all ten, save isolation, film-form chronology incl. Goku / Gogeta Blue, Full Power and Golden Frieza, God Bind, the melt and the lava stage unlock, the Dimension Break, the read / adaptation / Wrathful cues by real buttons, skip-safety, the RAGE ending, **all ten missions bot-played with every scene watched: success, 0 engine errors, 0 warnings, recorded on the film track**). Every checkpoint of every film mission is also started by the sim-tests regression sweep, and on the final code all **70 phase starts** of the ten missions (every phase, as the Developer Menu allows) ran clean: no engine error, the player never hidden while in control. **Full suite** (`node tests/run-tests.js`, once, on a snapshot taken before the last fixes): **799 passed, 2 failed** — both failures were older tests pinning a whole form list (Frieza's, Goku's) that the film's new forms extend; updated (the new list plus a lock check, their other assertions unchanged) and re-run on their own: pass. After the snapshot: `--fast` 51/0, the film tests 12/12, the 70-start sweep clean; the full suite was not run again. **Browser (Claude, smoke test):** the Movies screen (ten cards, lock chain), Part 1 launched through the menu, and direct starts of the Vampa beast, the melt, the dimension and the epilogue camp; four problems found and fixed there (the beast rising under the player's feet, the beast fight inside the ribcage set dressing, Gogeta invisible on checkpoint starts, the goodbye without its camp on a direct start). Not every shot was checked visually. **Not human playtested.**

### HUMAN PLAYTEST CHECKLIST — Movie 2
1. **Movies screen:** the film is listed as MOVIE 2 with the nine parts; the seven unbuilt ones say IN DEVELOPMENT and cannot be started; "The First Clash" opens once Chapter 16 is cleared.
2. **The arrival:** does the glacier read (white field, ice walls, the black channel, Frieza's ship) without washing out? Do the coats, Paragus' walk and Broly's first close-up land? Is "...Yes, Father." quiet enough?
3. **Vegeta vs base Broly:** Vegeta should feel faster and sharper at first. Throw the same strike again and again: is the CATCH (his open hand stopping your fist, a pause, the gut punch or the headbutt) readable — and is the on-screen lesson ("vary your attacks") clear? Does his meter (WATCHING → LEARNING → MATCHING) mean something to you? When he copies your strike, do you notice?
4. **Super Saiyan (hold Charge):** the pale green flicker before the gold; the ice breaking under the boots. Too long? Too short?
5. **Super Saiyan God (hold Charge):** the gold condensing to crimson, the leaner body, Goku's reaction.
6. **The God fight:** does God Vegeta feel dominant? Does watching the RAGE meter fill feel like a fuse burning down?
7. **The finish:** "Drive him into the ice" (Heavy) — the crater, Paragus shouting, the amber eyes in the dark.
8. **WRATH — the seven shots:** Fire (Blast) into the crater; the eyes; the rise; the glacier cracking; the fists and the veins; the silence, then the green aura; Brace (Guard); Dodge. Is every shot readable at normal speed? Any white-out? Is the silence before the aura long enough?
9. **SSG Vegeta vs Wrathful Broly:** is the change in him obvious (reach, the crashing leap with a red ring, roars, he does not flinch from light blows)? Is the collar beat (a free opening) noticed? Is it fair?
10. **Super Saiyan Blue, the fury, the clash:** does Broly catching up again read? Galick Gun vs Gigantic Roar: hold Special 1, release, mash — win or lose, does the outcome land?
11. **The end:** the slam, Goku's coat coming off, "my turn".

**Exact steps:** Settings → Developer / Test on; Main menu → Developer Menu → pick *MOVIE · The First Clash* or *MOVIE · Wrath* and a phase → Launch. Phases — First Clash: 0 · arrive, 1 · watch, 2 · gold, 3 · adapt, 4 · god, 5 · overwhelm, 6 · smash, 7 · after. Wrath: 0 · wake (the seven shots), 1 · rage, 2 · blue, 3 · fury, 4 · clash, 5 · end.

**Tuning knobs:** `SL.data.bbClash.CL` (`src/data/missions/bb_clash.js`: phase lengths, floors, Broly's dealt multipliers, `rageGain`), `SL.data.bbWrath.WR` (`bb_wrath.js`: phase lengths and minimums, Broly's dealt/taken, `collarAt`, health lost on a missed Brace/Dodge); Broly's reading per form: `forms[i].legendary` in `src/data/characters/broly.js` (`learnAt`, `blastAt`, `cooldown`, `gain`, `armor`), the stage thresholds `LEG_STAGES` in `src/game/mechanics.js`; his patterns per phase: `def.boss.phases[i].patterns`.

**Automated:** full suite `node tests/run-tests.js` 789 passed, 1 failed — an older test pinned Vegeta's form list without God/Blue; updated to the seven forms plus a check that God/Blue stay locked in Free Battle until the film gives them, passes (details: HANDOFF *Latest Test Results*); `tests/super-broly-tests.js` (9 tests, all pass on the final code); headless bot playthroughs of both missions (normal and every scene watched): success, 0 engine errors, 0 warnings. Bot pacing (seed 11): First Clash — watch 18 s, adapt 16 s, overwhelm 19 s (ends on RAGE); Wrath — rage 23 s, fury 21 s, clash. **Browser (Claude):** stepped 640x360 captures of every beat listed in the checklist; the white-out and framing problems found there are fixed (see HANDOFF). ## Movie 1: Fusion Reborn (2026-10-09) — FUNCTIONAL PROTOTYPE · AUTOMATED (FAST + HEADLESS BOTS) · BROWSER-CHECKED BY CLAUDE · HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED

Main menu → Movies (unlocks after "Fusion"). Seven missions + four challenges; see HANDOFF.md newest block for the file list.

### HUMAN PLAYTEST CHECKLIST
1. Opening: does the disaster read without narration (tank colour, lamps, gauge, Saike oblivious, explosion, Janemba growing out of the dark)?
2. The rifts on the flight in: readable, avoidable, not frustrating?
3. Pikkon at the barrier: are the three tests clear (strike, blast, heavy)? Does "force does nothing" land?
4. Fat Janemba's DIMENSION DOOR: is the tell (giggle, palms out, swirl) readable, and is "go round him or wait" learnable? Is a self-hit fair?
5. SSJ3 hold: hair growth attached to the scalp, no brows, full-body reveal.
6. Kamehameha finish: does holding Special 1 at point-blank feel like the finish? Is the Super Janemba evolution visible (not a model swap)?
7. Gohan vs Frieza: short, decisive, the one punch.
8. Tanks: do shells read (red line) and can you dodge them? Is knocking one into another satisfying?
9. Super Janemba: does Phantom Shift teach "vary your attacks"? Is the sword always visible and in his hand?
10. The fusion argument and the failed dance: is Vegeta's bent finger obvious?
11. Veku: is the bounce fun and the 30-minute ring clear? Too hard / too easy?
12. Pikkon's stand and defeat, the dance with Janemba approaching (see "Fusion Reborn final polish" steps 1–4). Gogeta (see also the final polish step 7 and the beat-sheet pass steps 4–7): reveal not overexposed? Evade cues, the two-finger catch, the shatter. **Stardust Breaker (rebuilt from the reference collage):** does holding Special 2 feel like it drives the shots? Is the face always clear of the raised arm? Does the sphere read as many colours in the wide hero shot and the toss? Is 5.4 s of holding too long? Does the purification read as the sphere's light filling Janemba (no explosion), the glitter, Saike landing?
13. Farewell: restrained, Vegeta fades, "Goodbye, my friend."

### Fusion Reborn final polish (2026-10-10) — AUTOMATED + BROWSER-CHECKED BY CLAUDE (paused frames; Pikkon's defeat also at normal speed) · HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED
Pikkon's playable stand and staged defeat (new `fr_gogeta` phase 1 · pikkon — **the Gogeta phases moved up by one**: 2 · dance, 3 · birth, 4 · dominate, 5 · finish); the second dance with Janemba approaching; Veku's last seconds and the "neither of us can take him alone" scene; readable armour chips and cracks; bigger, rimmed SSJ3 glass orbs; the dust's flight as a readable comet in two lenses with a hit beat; the two-finger catch solved before it is shown. Full suite: clean run after the last code edit, 2026-10-10: **779 passed, 0 failed** (777 before + the two new Fusion Reborn tests).

**Exact human playtest steps** (Settings → Developer / Test on; Main menu → Developer Menu → pick the mission and phase → Launch):
1. **Gogeta, phase 1 · pikkon** — the insults: *check* each one chips the chest plate (fragments, a violet ring, a crack left on the plate). Then you play Pikkon: hit Janemba with **Light (J)**, **Heavy (K)**, **Special 1 (1)**. *Check:* the blows land, chips fly off, the cracks build up on the plate, he does not flinch or fight back, his health bar barely moves.
2. After about six blows: the cut-in ("Nothing... My best blows...") and the new objective **Hold him off — block and evade** (24 s). Use **Guard (F)** and **Dodge**. *Check:* Janemba now attacks; the Dimension Slash joins at ~8 s and the claw out of a portal at ~16 s. **Is 24 s fair? Can a careful player stay standing (optional objective)?** (A non-blocking bot lasts 17–23 s.)
3. Still phase 1 — the defeat: *check* in order: the Hurricane Kick into empty air; Janemba re-forming behind him; the blocked cut that drives him back; the broken guard and the cleave that throws him across the field; the crash; "Not... yet...!"; the Thunder Flash going into a portal in front of Janemba and out of one behind Pikkon, into his back; the stillness; the failed push-up. Pikkon is alive (halo), Janemba turns toward the rock, "PIKKON!", Pikkon's warning, Vegeta and Goku. Too long? Any beat too fast to read?
4. **Gogeta, phase 2 · dance** — *check* Janemba is visible coming across the field behind them, nearer on every beat; miss a beat on purpose: his blade comes down between them from behind; on the retry he starts nearer.
5. **Veku, phase 3 · survive** (let the 75 s run out) — *check* the last seconds: Veku running for the rock, tripping onto his back, the sword going up over him, the pop, the blade hitting the ground between Goku and Vegeta, "The rock — MOVE!"; then the scene opens on Janemba hunting them, Vegeta's "Forget the dance..." and Goku's answer, and ends on Janemba turning toward the rock. (Phase 4 · timeup alone skips the last seconds: the fusion is not on.)
6. **Fat Janemba, phase 3 · ascend** — hold **Charge (R)**. *Check:* the glass orbs in the background are clearly visible and shatter during the scream (ring, falling shards), and one more in the silence before the burst; no white flash.
7. **Gogeta, phase 5 · finish** — the catch (**Guard (F)**): *check* the blade is visible up over Janemba's head at the cue, it comes down, it stops between two fingers near its point, Gogeta's face is calm and turned away, Janemba does not jump or slide. Then the Breaker (hold **2**, let go): after the crush, the shot from behind Janemba and the heel turn — *check* the dust's flight: rising over Gogeta against the dark sky, then chased into Janemba's chest; the hit (a ring of colours, the jolt, the frozen scream) before the light starts coming out of him. Purification, Saike, the smile, the farewell as before.
8. Write down any shot that is too short or too dark to read, anything hidden, and how the Pikkon stand felt (too long? too easy to get floored?).

Remaining limits: the Pikkon stand's balance is a bot-only judgement (non-blocking bot: 17–23 s of 24 on seeds 7/11/23); the plate cracks read up close and as dark marks at gameplay distance (~6.5 m), on the plate's front only; in the catch Gogeta ends turned ~49° off Janemba (the fit holds his turn and moves Janemba instead — a blind catch to his right side) and the blade meets the fingers near its point; the dust's flight is still fast (0.83 s, by design) and specks passing close to the chase lens blur in its last third; the defeat, Veku's last seconds and the dance re-place the fighters at fixed spots (each after a cut); between dance retries Janemba starts further out than where his blade landed; the SSJ3 burst is still the shared canon burst; `fr_x_super` remains hard.

### Fusion Reborn beat-sheet pass (2026-10-10, later) — AUTOMATED + BROWSER-CHECKED BY CLAUDE (paused frames; the finish also at normal speed) · HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED
The owner's seven-part beat sheet: SSJ3 shot the film's way; Goku in SSJ2 against Super Janemba, the drag, Vegeta kneeling with a bleeding shoulder, the needles and the leap; the Veku dance in mid-air under fire; Pikkon's insults buying the second dance; Gogeta's cold stare and the glide (Janemba's eye view); the five-part counter-combo; the Breaker crushed to dust, the shot from behind Janemba, the heel turn, the dust's own point of view; the eyes and mouth pouring light, the body coming apart, Saike's look round and look up, the yelp, the warm smile. Full suite: clean run after the last edit, 2026-10-10: **777 passed, 0 failed**.

**Exact human playtest steps** (Settings → Developer / Test on; Main menu → Developer Menu → pick the mission and phase → Launch):
1. **Fat Janemba, phase 3 · ascend** — hold **Charge (R)** to the full meter. *Check:* the teeth close-up; the scream with the elbows back and the head at the sky; blue lightning; the candy world shaking; glass orbs shattering in the background; the brow shot losing the eyebrows; the mane in slow motion; the silence; the burst. Too long? Too dark/bright?
2. **Nothing to Lose, phase 1 · struggle** — *check* Goku starts in **Super Saiyan 2** (gold, lightning) and SSJ3 is not offered. Let the duo run out (phase 3 · duo): *check* Goku visibly drags Vegeta away. Phase 4 · proposal: *check* Vegeta stays on one knee holding a bleeding shoulder all through the dance explanation; then Janemba overhead raining needles, "He found us — MOVE!", the leap.
3. **Veku, phase 1 · dance** — *check* they dance in the air with needles falling past and Janemba overhead; Light on every beat still reads; Veku drops out of the light and bounces. Phase 3 · survive: **is the 30-minute survival fair now?** (eased; bot survives 3 of 5 seeds).
4. **Gogeta, phase 1 · pikkon** (now followed by Pikkon's stand and defeat) and **phase 2 · dance** — *check* Pikkon in Janemba's face, the three insults, the armour chips, both flying off; the dance on the ground.
5. **Gogeta, phase 3 · birth** — *check* the reveal lines are stone-faced; the jaw shot (eyes looking down, no emotion); Janemba's eye view of the glide (legs still, feet off the ground).
6. **Gogeta, phase 5 · finish** — press the five cues: **Guard (F)** catch, **Light (J)** knee, **Heavy (K)** reverse kick, **Light** flurry, **Heavy** double palm. *Check:* the catch without looking (blade between the fingers), each blow, Janemba tumbling away, Gogeta turning his back. Then hold **Special 2 (2)** through the sphere, let go: *check* the crush into dust, the shot from behind Janemba, the heel turn, the backward dust flight, the hit in the chest.
7. Still in the finish — *check* the light out of his eyes and mouth, the body coming apart into dust floating up into a pink sky (no explosion), Saike falling and landing, looking around, looking up at Gogeta, the yelp, the scramble, Gogeta's warm smile.
8. Write down any shot that is too short to read at full speed, any frame where a face, the halo, the sphere or Janemba is hidden or cut off, and whether the five cues feel too slow (each waits up to 3 s).

Remaining limits (the first four were reworked in the final polish pass above): the armour chips in Pikkon's beat and the glass orbs in the SSJ3 scream are small at a distance; the dust's point-of-view shot is fast (0.7 s) and mostly against the bright ground; the catch sets Janemba a little closer so the blade meets the fingers; the SSJ3 burst itself is the shared canon burst; the Veku survival balance is a bot-only judgement; `fr_x_super` remains hard.

### Stardust Breaker follow-up (2026-10-10) — AUTOMATED + BROWSER-CHECKED BY CLAUDE (paused frames and wall-clock playback) · HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED
What changed: A and D refitted for the face (A 25 → 29 px, D 24 → 26 px at 640x360; head toward the lens); the throw is now over Gogeta's shoulder into one continuous Janemba shot through the purification; a clean cut into A (no hand-off flash: `switchPlayer({ quiet: true })`); **cues and rhythms now take the real button when a scene scripts the player body** (the Gogeta combination never took a press before). Full suite: clean run after the last edit, 2026-10-10: **777 passed, 0 failed**.

**Exact human playtest steps (about 5 minutes):**
1. Settings → Developer / Test → turn the mode on (it uses a separate test save; your real progress is untouched). Back to the main menu → Developer Menu.
2. Mission: **Gogeta** (`fr_gogeta`). Phase: **5 · finish**. Launch. (For the whole film's end instead: phase 1 · pikkon ◆ or 2 · dance ◆, or, on a normal save, Main menu → Movies → Fusion Reborn → Gogeta once the film's earlier missions are cleared.)
3. The combination: press **Light** (J or left mouse) on "Strike", "Strike", then **Heavy** (K or right mouse) on "Finish the combination". *Check:* each press is taken at once (no 3-second wait).
4. Shot A (the reveal): *check* the halo, the whole body, his face readable, no white flash on the cut. Wait a second or two without pressing — nothing should happen until you press.
5. Press and **hold Special 2** (keyboard **2**). *Check:* the arm goes up, deep space comes in, the sphere grows over his palm through shots B–F; his face is never covered by the arm; the sphere reads as many colours in the wide hero shot D. Let go once halfway: *check* the shot and the sphere simply wait; hold again to continue.
6. When the prompt says to let go (full sphere, his face shot F), **release 2**. *Check:* over his shoulder the sphere flies to Janemba; the cut to Janemba; it sinks into his chest; the light swells in him (no explosion, no white frame); the glitter rises (over Gogeta's shoulder); Saike drops out, frightened; the sky comes back; the smirk; the farewell.
7. Optional: start the phase again and press nothing for 15 s — the Breaker should play itself (a lower QTE score); hold 2 and never let go — it should throw itself 5 s after the sphere is full.
8. Write down: is 5.4 s of holding too long? Is any shot too short to read at full speed? Any frame where the face, the halo or the sphere is cut off?

Remaining limits: full-body faces stay small at low resolutions (geometry: the frame must hold ~3 m); the sash tails (his left hip) barely show from the right-side lenses; the combination's own camera (before the Breaker) sometimes puts Janemba in the foreground — untouched here; the stage props inside the deep-space radius (lollipops, jellybeans, floating blocks) stay visible as silhouettes by design.

### Stardust Breaker rebuild (2026-10-09) — BROWSER-CHECKED BY CLAUDE (stepped frames) · HUMAN PLAYTEST REQUIRED
Seven shots (A reveal, B gathering, C sphere vs dark, D hero full body, E sphere close-up, F face + sphere, G toss into his chest), then the purification with the same sphere inside Janemba. New: `SL.Dimension.prismOrb`, `SL.Dimension.cosmos`, poses `gg_breakerStand` / `gg_breakerRaise`, model `wind`, halo in full-shot framing, aura view 0 silences its particles. Knobs: `SL.data.frGogeta.GG.charge` (5.4 s), `orbR` (0.3 m), `releaseAuto` (5 s), `throwAuto` (14 s). Full suite 2026-10-09 (clean run after the last edit): 772 passed, 3 failed — all three are stale tests from earlier passes (Vegeta SSJ lab timing; Vegeta and Goku form lists that predate super_vegeta and god/godki). The run also caught and fixed two Fusion Reborn regressions (Snake Way builder override, Janemba utility techniques in the coverage test). Known limits: small face in the full-body shots at 640x360; sash tails barely visible from the right-side lenses; real-time playback not watched in a visible window.

### Tuning knobs
`SL.data.frJanemba.JB`, `frVegeta.VG`, `frVeku.VK`, `frGogeta.GG`, `frGohan.GH`, `frBoys.BY/TANKS`, `frCleansing.CL`; Janemba `def.dimension`; Veku `def.blimp`.

## Testing record: AUTOMATED TESTED vs HUMAN PLAYTESTED

**AUTOMATED TESTED** means verified by the Node test suite, bots, probes, CPU-vs-CPU runs or Claude driving the game in a browser. It says nothing about how the game feels. **HUMAN PLAYTESTED** means a person actually played it and reported the result. Automated results never count as human playtesting, and a problem a human reported stays "awaiting human retest" until a human has played the fix.
